# CG-IR-PRICEBOOK-SPEC@0.1.0: Official Price Book & Item Master Specification

| Field             | Value                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Spec ID           | `CG-IR-PB`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Version           | `0.1.0`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Status            | Draft. Specification only — except the official 1404 ابنیه edition, which ships as a verified, hash-bound staged dataset (see `packages/pricebook/README.md`); the edition import/approval pipeline and multi-edition Item Master are not implemented |
| Scope             | Official base price lists for building works (فهرست بهای واحد پایه رشته ابنیه), starting with the 1404 edition                                                                                                                                        |
| Synthetic fixture | [`fixtures/synthetic-edition.v0.1.0.json`](fixtures/synthetic-edition.v0.1.0.json)                                                                                                                                                                    |

## 0. Source policy (normative)

1. **No price, item code, description, coefficient or rule may be typed in, generated or inferred.** Every record enters
   from an official source file through the pipeline in §5 and keeps a `SourceRef`.
2. The official 1404 ابنیه price list is the target first edition. Its identity is verified (§3.1): `فهرست بهای واحد پایه رشته ابنیه سال ۱۴۰۴`, edition `1404`, circular `1403/742948` dated `1403/12/29`. Per-fact printed-page citations are in §3.2.
   The file hash and any metadata not listed in §3.1 (e.g. publisher wording, effective date) must still be recorded **from the actual file**.
3. The legacy sample financial and takeoff workbooks are **workflow benchmarks only**. They are based on an older edition, and nothing is imported from them.
4. AI may help extract tables from a source file into **staging**. Staged rows are unverified until a human approves them, and AI cannot publish (D-008).

## 1. Chain of concepts (normative separation)

```
Takeoff Quantity (S1) → Item Master (S2) → Price Book (S3) → Raw Amount (S3)
  → Estimate / Coefficients (S4) → Final Estimate (S4)
```

| Concept                     | What it is                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Owns prices?                    | Package                                                |
| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Item Master**             | Identity of a work item across editions: code, description, unit, chapter, lineage (new/changed/removed)                                                                                                        | **No**                          | `pricebook`                                            |
| **Price Book**              | One immutable edition: `(item, basePrice)` for a given edition, plus its instructions and chapter notes                                                                                                         | **Yes** (base unit prices only) | `pricebook`                                            |
| **Raw Amount**              | Row amount = quantity × unit price; chapter amount = Σ row amounts (verified, §3.2 F4–F5). Exact; rounding not verified                                                                                         | derived                         | `calc-engine` (S3, future)                             |
| **Estimate / Coefficients** | Floor coefficient, overhead coefficient, regional coefficient and site setup/removal costs, applied per the applicable rules (verified, §3.2 F6); rules recorded in §11 as VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY (not implemented) | No (definitions only)           | `calc-engine` (S4, future), definitions in `pricebook` |
| **Final Estimate**          | Locked estimate version                                                                                                                                                                                         | derived                         | `projects`                                             |

The generic quantity engine never imports price-book data.

## 2. Common types

```
SourceRef {
  sourceDocument: string        // exact title as printed on the source
  edition: string               // e.g. "1404"
  page: string | null           // as printed; null if unknown
  section: string | null        // chapter / clause / table id as printed
  sourceFileId: string          // stored original file
  sourceFileHash: string        // sha256 of original file
  locator?: { row?: number, table?: string }  // extraction locator
}
VerificationStatus = "unverified" | "verified" | "rejected" | "superseded"
Money = IRR decimal string (domain Money); never float
```

## 3. Edition

```
PriceBookEdition {
  editionId
  discipline: "abniye"                      // V1 scope (an earlier owner decision; not D-015)
  year: string                              // Jalali year as published, e.g. "1404"
  title: string                             // as printed
  publication: {
    publisher: string                       // as printed
    notificationNo: string | null           // بخشنامه number, as printed
    notificationDate: string | null         // Jalali, as printed
    effectiveFrom: string | null            // as stated by source; else null
  }
  sourceFiles: { sourceFileId, fileName, sha256, pages, receivedAt, receivedBy }[]
  status: "staging" | "validated" | "approved" | "published" | "withdrawn"
  importerVersion, publishedAt?, publishedBy?, approvals: Approval[]
  contentHash?                              // sha256 of canonical published content
}
```

A published edition is **immutable**. A correction is a new edition (e.g. an erratum edition) that references the one it `supersedes`.
Withdrawing an edition never deletes it, because estimates that reference it stay reproducible.

## 4. Edition content: separate concepts

Each of the following is a distinct record type with its own `SourceRef` and `VerificationStatus`. Their **content is
not specified here**. It comes only from the source file.

| #   | Concept (per F1)                                       | Record                                                                       | Notes                                                                                                                                            |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| C1a | Application instructions (دستورالعمل کاربرد)           | `EditionInstruction { id, title, text, clauses[] }`                          | Source of F2–F9                                                                                                                                  |
| C1b | General requirements (مقررات/کلیات عمومی)              | `GeneralRequirement { id, text, clauses[] }`                                 | Measurement is subject to these (F7); only clause 10 verified (IR-1404-E-GENERAL-10-01)                                                          |
| C2  | Chapter requirements and chapters (فصل)                | `Chapter { chapterNo, title, requirements: ChapterRequirement[] }`           | Measurement is subject to these (F7); only Ch. 7 cl. 2, Ch. 8 cl. 6 (measurement spec) and Ch. 28 (§11) verified                                 |
| C3  | Item descriptions and unit prices                      | `PriceItem { itemCode, chapterNo, description, unit, basePrice, itemClass }` | `itemClass`: `"base"` or `"star-new"` (F8); `unit` = printed label + mapped `UnitCode` (§6)                                                      |
| C4  | Appendix 1: on-site materials (مصالح پای کار)          | `AppendixRecord { appendixNo: 1, ... }`                                      | Verified in §11: IR-1404-E-ONSITE-01..07, ONSITE-T2-01/02 (§11.3, §11.5); interim payment statements only                                        |
| C5  | Appendix 2: floor coefficient (ضریب طبقات)             | `CoefficientDefinition { kind: "floor", appendixNo: 2 }`                     | Applied in S4 (F6). Unrelated to takeoff `floorCount`                                                                                            |
| C6  | Appendix 3: overhead-cost items (هزینه‌های بالاسری)    | `CoefficientDefinition { kind: "overhead", appendixNo: 3 }`                  | Descriptive only (IR-1404-E-OVERHEAD-COST-01..05); coefficient values come from Application Instructions cl. 2-7-2 (OVERHEAD-01), not Appendix 3 |
| C7  | Appendix 4: regional coefficient (ضریب منطقه‌ای)       | `CoefficientDefinition { kind: "regional", appendixNo: 4 }`                  | Applied in S4 (F6); values external (IR-1404-E-REGION-01)                                                                                        |
| C8  | Appendix 5: site setup/removal (تجهیز و برچیدن کارگاه) | `SiteSetupDefinition { appendixNo: 5 }`                                      | Applied in S4 (F6); lump sum capped by percentage (IR-1404-E-SITE-01/02)                                                                         |
| C9  | Appendix 6: new works (کارهای جدید)                    | `NewWorkRule { appendixNo: 6 }`                                              | Star/new items are a separate concept (F8); IR-1404-E-NEW-01..04; Article 29 content unverified                                                  |

```
CoefficientDefinition {
  id, kind: "floor" | "regional" | "overhead"     // the three coefficient kinds named in F6
  appendixNo: 2 | 3 | 4
  title, text                                // verbatim/faithful
  structure: "unstructured"                  // 0.1.0: stored as text only; machine structure added per verified kind
  source: SourceRef, status: VerificationStatus
}
```

In 0.1.0 **no coefficient is machine-applicable**. Structured forms (tables, conditions, application scope) will be
specified per kind only after a domain expert has verified the source text (Q-PB-3).

## 5. Pipeline: source → staging → validation → approval → publish

```
1. INGEST     original file stored; sha256 computed; metadata recorded by a named user
2. EXTRACT    importer (versioned) or assisted extraction → StagingRow[] (each with locator + raw text)
3. NORMALISE  digits (Persian/Arabic-Indic → ASCII), separators removed, unit label → UnitCode via mapping table;
              raw value always preserved alongside normalised value
4. VALIDATE   rules §7 → ValidationReport (errors block; warnings require acknowledgement)
5. REVIEW     human reviewer checks rows against source pages (sampling plan + all flagged rows); edits are audited
6. APPROVE    ≥ 1 approver with role Data Steward, distinct from the importer user (four-eyes)
7. PUBLISH    canonical content frozen, contentHash computed, status = published; audit event emitted
```

Every step emits an audit event: `{ actor, at, editionId, step, before?, after?, origin: user | import | ai-assisted }`.
Staging rows edited by a human keep their original extracted value and the edit reason.

## 6. Unit label mapping

`UnitMapping { editionId, printedLabel, unitCode, status, source }`. The printed label is stored verbatim. Mapping it to a
`UnitCode` is an approval-gated decision, never guessed. Unmapped labels block publication.

## 7. Validation rules

| ID     | Rule                                                                                                                                                      | Severity |
| ------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- |
| PB-V1  | `itemCode` unique within edition. Numbers are structured by chapter/group/subchapter (F9), but no format parser is specified until the layout is verified | error    |
| PB-V2  | Every item belongs to an existing chapter                                                                                                                 | error    |
| PB-V3  | `basePrice` is a non-negative integer Rial decimal string, or explicitly marked "no price" if the source prints none                                      | error    |
| PB-V4  | Unit label mapped (§6)                                                                                                                                    | error    |
| PB-V5  | Every record has a complete `SourceRef` (`sourceDocument`, `edition`, `sourceFileHash`; `page`/`section` or explicit null with reason)                    | error    |
| PB-V6  | Chapter item counts / totals match any control figures printed in the source (if present)                                                                 | error    |
| PB-V7  | Description non-empty; raw text preserved                                                                                                                 | error    |
| PB-V8  | Duplicates / near-duplicate descriptions within chapter                                                                                                   | warning  |
| PB-V9  | Diff vs previous edition: new, removed, unit-changed, description-changed items listed for review                                                         | warning  |
| PB-V10 | No record has `status = verified` without a verifier and timestamp                                                                                        | error    |

## 8. Item Master lineage

`ItemMasterEntry { masterId, currentCode, history: { editionId, itemCode, description, unit, change: "new" | "same" | "changed" | "removed" }[] }`.
Lineage links are proposals until a human confirms them. A code that is reused across editions is **not** assumed to mean the same item.

## 9. Consumption contract (for S2/S3)

- S2 mapping `takeoff itemCode → PriceItem` requires a **published** edition, and the takeoff unit must equal the item's `unitCode`.
  Errors: `UNKNOWN_ITEM_CODE`, `ITEM_UNIT_MISMATCH`, `EDITION_NOT_PUBLISHED`.
- S3 resolves `basePrice` and returns `{ value, editionId, itemCode, source }`. Overrides are layered on top (D-006) and
  never mutate the edition.

## 11. Verified Iranian Rules — 1404 (estimation components)

Source file: `فهرست بهای واحد پایه رشته ابنیه سال ۱۴۰۴` (repository copy on `main`), sha256 `c49e315548152da03d54394ca46b558592817de1ad24b29392d84311216fae0f`.
Printed page N = PDF page index N + 2 in this file.

Rule statements are English renderings of the official text, verified visually against the rendered PDF pages (the PDF text layer
corrupts digits, so every number was checked on the page image). A verbatim Persian transcription is still pending.
`VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY` means the rule is recorded and sourced but **not implemented**: it changes no number in any engine until
a later phase implements it with its own reference cases. Sample-workbook values are never used as 1404 rules.

Concept separation (normative): **Item Master**, **Price Book**, **Measurement Rules** (measurement spec §9),
**Coefficients** (§4 C5–C7) and **Estimate calculations** (S4) are distinct. None of the rules below is placed in the quantity engine.

| IR-1404-E-REGION-01        | فهرست بهای واحد پایه رشته ابنیه سال ۱۴۰۴ | 1404    | 243         | Appendix 4 — Regional Coefficient (ضریب منطقه‌ای), clauses 1, 1-1, 1-2, 1-3, 1-4                                                                                          | Prices in this price list are based on labour, machinery, materials and transport prices with easy access to materials and services; to compensate additional costs arising from the distribution of infrastructure, climate, geography, labour and distance from main centres, a regional coefficient is determined and applied in the estimate of execution cost (1). The regional coefficients used are the latest coefficients issued, up to the time the estimate is prepared, by the Plan and Budget Organization in the annex to circular No. 94/69416 dated 1394/04/30 or its later amendments (1-1). If the project location's name is not among the areas for which a coefficient is set in that circular or its amendments, the regional coefficient of the county or district (شهرستان یا بخش) in which the project is located is used (1-2). Geographic boundaries of province, county and district follow the latest national divisions map published by the Ministry of Interior (1-3). For projects located in different regions, e.g. linear projects, R = [(R1×C1) + (R2×C2) + … + (Rn×Cn)] / C, where R = regional coefficient of the discipline (رشته), C = estimated execution cost of the work of that discipline, Ci = estimated execution cost of the part of the work to which regional coefficient Ri applies (1-4). | Estimate of execution cost (S4); per discipline (رشته) — weighted average by cost where the project spans several regions                                           | All coefficient values and the region/location mapping: they are not printed in this document but in the annex to circular 94/69416 (1394/04/30) and later amendments, which is not an available source; verbatim text | VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY   |

Origins: cement — the nearest domestic cement plant producing the required cement at supply time (1-2-1); stone materials — the nearest production place producing it at supply time (1-3-1); steel bought directly from domestic producers or the commodity exchange — the production place or the exchange delivery place (1-4-1), otherwise the nearest steel producer (1-4-2); brick, clay block and light block — the production place (1-5-1). Bulk cement (سیمان فله) is priced from the related rows with coefficient 1.15 for the whole route after deducting 30 km (1-2-2). Cold-rolled galvanised steel materials/parts: rows with coefficient 1.20, origin the producing plant (1-4-3). Steel for structures fabricated in a factory: beyond-30-km cost from supply place to the factory per 1-4-4, and loading/transport/unloading of fabricated parts from factory to site from the related row without deducting the first 30 km, with coefficient 2 (1-4-5). The per-unit material quantities of Group 1 (e.g. 1-2-3 to 1-2-10, 1-3-2 to 1-3-13, 1-4-4, 1-5-2 to 1-5-5) are transport-quantity rules only (clause 5); recorded as IR-1404-E-TRANSPORT-QTY-01 (§11.7).            | Cement, stone materials, steel, brick and block only                                                                                                                | Group 1 per-unit transport quantities (not transcribed); verbatim text                                                                                                                                                 | VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY   |

Sea-transport rows are measured in تن - مایل دریایی (tonne-nautical mile) and include all loading, transport, unloading and related charges (5-1). ### 11.1 Floor coefficient P (IR-1404-E-FLOOR-01..03)

Verified formula (printed page 239, clause 1-3; checked visually):

```
P = 1 + [(1×F1 + 2×F2 + … + n×Fn) + (1×B1 + 2×B2 + … + m×Bm)] / (100 × S)

F0 = ground floor area (همکف)            Fi = area of the i-th floor above the ground floor
B0 = area of the floor below the ground floor (زیر همکف)
Bj = area of the j-th floor below B0
S  = total floor area of the building, including ground floor, B0, all floors above the ground floor and all floors below B0
Rounding: IR-1404-E-FLOOR-02 (4 decimals; 5th decimal < 5 → drop, ≥ 5 → +1 on the 4th)
```

F0 and B0 carry no weight in the numerator but are included in S.

Worked example (printed page 240; checked visually). This will be the first S4 reference case:

```json
{
  "belowB0": [
    { "level": "B1", "area": "400" },
    { "level": "B2", "area": "400" },
    { "level": "B3", "area": "400" }
  ],
  "B0": "400",
  "F0": "600",
  "aboveF0": [
    { "level": "F1", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F2", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F3", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F4", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F5", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F6", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F7", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F8", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F9", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F10", "area": "500" },
    { "level": "F11", "area": "400" }
  ],
  "S": "7600",
  "weightedSum": "34300",
  "unrounded": "1.045132",
  "P": "1.0451"
}
```

The source prints the intermediate value as 0.045132 (six decimals), which is what the "unrounded" field records. The exact quotient is 1.0451315…

### 11.6 Chapter 28 — transport rows (IR-1404-E-TRANSPORT-01..04)

Printed pages 231–232. Values read from the rendered page images; the text layer agrees on all 13 rates once its digit corruption
(printed ۰ appears as 1) is taken into account. Descriptions are abbreviated; road rows 280101–280106 and 280301–280302 all read

Relation to Appendix 1 (IR-1404-E-ONSITE-03): both provisions use the same 30 km inclusion. Beyond 30 km, the source pays transport
only for the materials covered by these rows (cement, stone materials, steel, brick and block); for other materials no separate transport
cost is paid (IR-1404-E-TRANSPORT-01). Which Table 2 row falls into which category is not stated by the source and is not mapped here.

### 11.2 Notes

- **Overhead:** construction overhead values (IR-1404-E-OVERHEAD-01) map only to their stated classifications. The purchase-chapter
  1.14 (OVERHEAD-02) and the equipment-only new-work 1.14 (NEW-03) are separate and must not be used as ordinary construction overhead.
- **Table A** (printed page 2, clause 2-7-5) summarises the star-item caps 30 / 15 / 10 percent; they are recorded from clause 2-6 as IR-1404-E-STAR-02.
- **Clause 2-8** runs from printed page 2 (BOQ row, row amount, chapter amount; F2–F6 in §3.2) to page 3 (application of coefficients).
- **On-site materials** (IR-1404-E-ONSITE-01) belong to interim payment statements, which are out of V1 scope.
- **Rounding separation:** IR-1404-E-FLOOR-02 is the only verified source rounding rule. It is an estimation-coefficient rule and
  stays separate from the generic engine rounding policies. No global Iranian rounding policy is assumed.
- **Still UNVERIFIED:**
  - contents of Article 29(a)/(c) of the General Conditions of Contract;
  - regional coefficient values (Appendix 4 refers to circular 94/69416 and amendments; no values printed in the 1404 PDF);
  - price for Appendix 1 Table 2 row 411004 (not printed); UNRESOLVED. Phase 3.5-U: p238 rechecked visually (unit کیلوگرم,
    price cell blank); full-PDF search found no other 411004 rate, no row named as its source and no pricing formula. Appendix 1
    clause 4 covers items with no Table 2 row, not a row with a blank price, so it is not a fallback for 411004. Chapter 7 rows for
    prestressing cable (p60) are supply-and-installation work rows and are not linked to 411004 by the source. No external source
    is named. The engine must not invent, estimate, zero or N/A-price it; any amount needing 411004 stays unvalued;
  - mapping of Appendix 1 Table 2 rows to Chapter 28 transport categories (NO_EXPLICIT_APPENDIX_1_MAPPING);
  - the star-row preparation and approval instruction referenced by clause 2-6 (NOT_FOUND_IN_PROJECT_SOURCES;
    EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY). Phase 3.5-T: p2, clause 2-6 prints only the title «دستورالعمل نحوه تهیه و تصویب ردیف‌های ستاره‌دار»; no
    document number, date or issuing authority is printed. Chapter requirements refer to it as «دستورالعمل اقلام ستاره‌دار» (for
    example pp. 9, 62, 233), also without number or date. No copy exists in the project; no procedural detail is taken from it;
  - verbatim Persian transcriptions.

### 11.8 Appendix 1 Table 2 ↔ Chapter 28 mapping verification (Phase 3.5-S)

Full-PDF search (text layer of all 258 pages; printed pages 228–238 checked visually) for any link between Table 2 rows
410202–413102 and Chapter 28 groups, quantity clauses 1-2-3 … 1-5-5 or rows 280101–280505:

| finding                                                                                                                                                                  | page    | classification                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Appendix 1 clause 2: Table 2 prices include loading, transport ≤ 30 km and unloading; no extra transport beyond 30 km except cases provided for in the transport chapter | 235     | PARTIALLY_SPECIFIED (general link only; names no row, group or quantity clause) |
| Chapter 28 (pp. 228–232) contains no reference to Appendix 1, مصالح پای‌کار or Table 2                                                                                   | 228–232 | NOT_SPECIFIED                                                                   |
| No other page cross-references Appendix 1 Table 2 to Chapter 28 (the only other Chapter 28 reference, p29, concerns Chapter 3 fill material)                             | all     | NOT_SPECIFIED                                                                   |

Result: 0 explicit row mappings; 1 partial general link (clause 2); all 46 Table 2 rows have no deterministic Chapter 28
category, quantity basis or rate family in the 1404 source. Table 2 rates (§11.5) and Chapter 28 rules/quantities (§11.6–§11.7)
are each verified separately. The engine must not infer the mapping from material names; beyond-30-km transport for a Table 2
row stays unavailable until an authoritative source establishes the relationship. No external document is referenced for it.

Phase 3.5-AD recheck: none of the 46 Table 2 row numbers appears anywhere in the PDF outside Table 2 (pp. 237–238); Chapter 28
(pp. 228–232) contains no reference to Appendix 1, Table 2, on-site materials or prestressing cable; no external mapping
document is named. Chapter 28 Group 1 names its categories only by material words (سیمان, مصالح سنگی, آهن‌آلات, آجر و بلوک,
with the special cases bulk cement, cold-rolled galvanised steel and factory-fabricated steel) and never by Table 2 row. No
special material in Table 2 (bulk cement, galvanised sheets, aluminium, timber, paint, gypsum, lime, bitumen, lightweight
aggregate, burlap, wire/Rabitz mesh, 411004) has an explicit link. Verified row mappings: 0. Unresolved: all 46.
Classification: UNRESOLVED. Name similarity (for example sand → stone, cement → cement, rebar → steel) is not evidence; an
unmapped row gets no Chapter 28 amount, and a missing Chapter 28 category never means that transport is free.

### 11.9 Regional coefficient: rule vs values (Phase 3.5-V)

Appendix 4 (p243) was rechecked visually. The full-PDF text search found no regional coefficient table or value anywhere in the 1404
PDF; the other mentions (pp. 1–2, 235) only name the coefficient.

| item                        | status                           | basis                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| rule (IR-1404-E-REGION-01)  | VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY               | clauses 1, 1-1 to 1-4, p243: formula `R = Σ(Ri × Ci) / C` per discipline; county/district (شهرستان یا بخش) fallback; boundaries per the latest Ministry of Interior divisions map |
| regional coefficient values | EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY / UNRESOLVED | not printed in the 1404 PDF; clause 1-1 requires the latest coefficients issued up to the time the estimate is prepared                                                           |
| external source identity    | as printed on p243               | annex to circular No. 94/69416 dated 1394/04/30 or its later amendments, issued by سازمان برنامه و بودجه کشور; no title is printed                                                |

- The 1394 circular is not automatically the applicable source: the latest amendment issued up to the estimate preparation date
  governs. Source selection needs the estimate preparation date, project location, discipline and a sourced value set.
- No authoritative value set or amendment list was verified (Phase 3.5-V web check: only third-party copies were found; the
  official site could not be reached). No regional value is recorded here or in the engine.
- The fallback is only the county/district coefficient from that source; never a nearest city, province average, national
  value or arbitrary user value. If the source has no value for the required unit, the value is missing (INCOMPLETE).
- Phase 3.5-AC classification (separate dependencies; p243 rechecked; project and official site searched again):

  | dependency                                  | classification      | note                                                                                                  |
  | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | methodology (Appendix 4 rules)              | VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY  | 1404 PDF p243 only                                                                                    |
  | numerical coefficient table                 | EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY | annex to 94/69416 not obtained from an official source; third-party copies are not used               |
  | later amendments / version current for 1404 | UNRESOLVED          | no amendment identified; the latest source up to the estimate preparation date cannot be determined   |
  | administrative boundaries (clause 1-3)      | EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY | latest Ministry of Interior national divisions map; separate from the coefficient table; not obtained |

- The estimate preparation date is a required input for selecting the coefficient version (clause 1-1). Geographic
  granularity, discipline split, rounding or extra multi-region rules of the external table are not verified and are not
  recorded. No regional lookup is implemented or claimed as verified.

## 12. Calculation Engine Implementation Contract

Normative for the future S3/S4 implementation. **Nothing in this section is implemented.** It restates §11 (and measurement spec §9.3)
in machine-oriented form. It adds no rule, value or fallback. Source pages are printed pages.

### 12.1 Status labels

| label                                   | meaning                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY`                    | recorded from the 1404 PDF; not implemented (all §11 rules)    |
| `IMPLEMENTABLE_FROM_CURRENT_SOURCE`     | the source fully defines the operation given the listed inputs |
| `EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY`                   | needs a document outside the 1404 PDF                          |
| `NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_FROM_CURRENT_SOURCE` | the source does not define the operation                       |
| `UNRESOLVED`                            | a value or rule is missing or open in the source               |

Types: **A** computational, **B** input/lookup, **C** validation/constraint, **D** payment/accounting, **E** descriptive,
**F** external dependency, **G** unresolved. Each rule has exactly one type.

Engine outcome vocabulary (engine behaviour, not pricebook rules; kept distinct):

| outcome                           | meaning                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ERROR`                           | a required input is missing or makes the formula undefined; nothing is computed                       |
| `INCOMPLETE`                      | a required external value (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY) is not supplied; the dependent amount is not computed |
| `NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK` | the source is silent; the engine does not compute and does not choose an interpretation               |
| `EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY`             | status label only (§12.1); at runtime it surfaces as `INCOMPLETE`                                     |

Zero, missing, negative and not-applicable are distinct inputs. "Not applicable" (for example no regional split) is an explicit
input, never inferred from a missing or zero value. Input-validation limits that the source does not state (for example negative
areas) are engine input validation, not pricebook rules, and are labelled as such.

### 12.3 Flow contracts

All arithmetic is exact (no floating point). Rounding is applied only where a rule states it. Otherwise the generic engine policies
(CG-RCS) apply, never an invented Iranian rule.

**A. Basic rows (FLOW-01, clause 2-8, pp. 2–3).**

- `rowAmount = quantity × unitPrice`.
- `chapterAmount = Σ rowAmount` over the chapter.
- `priceListTotal = Σ chapterAmount`.
- Star (non-base) rows are rows like any other in these sums; they carry `itemClass = "star-new"` (STAR-01).
- Site setup/removal is not a row of these sums.
- Rounding of row, chapter and total amounts is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.

**B. Floor coefficient P (FLOOR-01..03, pp. 239–240).**

- Formula: `P = 1 + [(1×F1+…+n×Fn) + (1×B1+…+m×Bm)] / (100 × S)`.
- F0 and B0 get no weight but are included in S.
- S = total floor area of the building (F0, B0, all Fi, all Bj).
- P is computed per building and applies only to that building's work items. It excludes landscaping and on-site materials.
- Rounding: 4 decimals, half-up on the 5th decimal (FLOOR-02), applied to the exact quotient.
- Reference case: S = 7600, weighted sum 34300 → P = 1.0451.
- More than one ground level: the main ground floor in the initial drawings is F0 (FLOOR-01 Note 1); this is an input, never inferred.
- S = 0: the formula is undefined → `ERROR`. Negative areas: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK; refused by engine input
  validation (not a pricebook rule). Fractional areas are exact decimals.
- Missing floor data → `ERROR`. The engine never defaults P to 1 and never guesses areas. A building with no floors above or
  below F0/B0 has P = 1 only because the formula yields it from supplied areas, never as a default.
- Recalculation (FLOOR-03) belongs to contract execution and is out of V1 scope.

**C. Overhead (OVERHEAD-01/02, NEW-03, GENERAL-10-01).**

- Lookup by `(planType, tenderRoute)`, clause 2-7-2, p2:

  | planType                | tender route                                       | coefficient |
  | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ----------- |
  | capital (عمرانی)        | tender, or no tender due to monopoly               | 1.30        |
  | capital                 | formalities waived, or no tender for other reasons | 1.20        |
  | non-capital (غیرعمرانی) | tender, or no tender due to monopoly               | 1.41        |
  | non-capital             | formalities waived, or no tender for other reasons | 1.30        |

- No default classification: a missing input → `ERROR`.
- 1.14 is used only for:
  - ابنیه purchase items = clause 10 fittings (OVERHEAD-02, GENERAL-10-01);
  - officially approved equipment-only new work (NEW-03).
- Appendix 3 (OVERHEAD-COST-01..05) is descriptive and never a coefficient source.

**D. Regional coefficient (REGION-01, p243).**

- Formula: `R = Σ(Ri × Ci) / C`, per discipline (رشته). C = Σ Ci of that discipline.
- Every Ri must be supplied from the external circular 94/69416 and its amendments, with a source reference.
- The engine holds no regional values.
- Location not listed in the circular: the county/district (شهرستان یا بخش) coefficient of that circular is used (REGION-01 1-2); it
  is still an external input, never a province value chosen by the engine.
- A missing Ri or Ci → `INCOMPLETE`. C = 0 → `ERROR` (formula undefined). Never use 1 or 0 in place of Ri.
- R is per discipline only; it is never a project-global coefficient across disciplines.

**E. Application order (FLOW-01).**

- The floor, overhead and regional coefficients are multiplied successively ("به صورت پی در پی") onto the sum of row amounts, each only
  where applicable ("بر حسب مورد"). The site setup/removal cost is then added.
- Scope and aggregation (Phase 3.5-AA; pp. 2–3, 239, 243, 235, 254 rechecked):

  | item                                                  | source wording (summary)                                                                                                                         | status                                        |
  | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------- |
  | chain (clause 2-8, p2)                                | P, overhead and regional (بر حسب مورد) multiplied successively onto the sum of row amounts; site setup/removal then added                        | VERIFIED_FROM_1404_SOURCE (single-scope case) |
  | several disciplines (Application Instructions 7, p3)  | each discipline's estimate prepared separately under its own price list; a summary sheet lists them separately and as a sum; one site setup list | VERIFIED_FROM_1404_SOURCE                     |
  | P scope (Appendix 2 clause 1-3, p239)                 | calculated separately for each building (excluding landscaping items) and applied to all work items of that building; not on-site materials      | VERIFIED_FROM_1404_SOURCE                     |
  | R scope (Appendix 4 clause 1-4, p243)                 | one R per discipline; Ci = estimate of the part with Ri, C = estimate of that discipline                                                         | VERIFIED_FROM_1404_SOURCE                     |
  | overhead (clause 2-7-2, p2)                           | value by plan type and tender route; no row-subset scope stated beyond the purchase-item column of Table A                                       | VERIFIED_FROM_1404_SOURCE (value only)        |
  | contractor's proposed coefficient                     | named only in ONSITE-05 (p235) and NEW-02 (p254) "as applicable"; not part of the 1404 price-list chain                                          | out of scope; no general scope stated         |
  | several buildings with different P in one discipline  | no method for combining per-building amounts with the clause 2-8 chain                                                                           | UNRESOLVED                                    |
  | R basis for C and Ci (before or after P / overhead)   | not stated                                                                                                                                       | UNRESOLVED                                    |
  | rows outside a coefficient's scope (e.g. landscaping) | no method for combining with in-scope rows in the chain                                                                                          | UNRESOLVED                                    |

- The engine never applies a coefficient outside its verified scope, never uses one P for several buildings, never uses one R
  across disciplines, and never writes `Σ rows × P × overhead × R` or `Σ(row × all coefficients)` for a mixed-scope estimate. Such
  an estimate returns NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK for the combined amount; per-discipline sums and the summary sheet stay
  computable where each discipline is single-scope.
- No other coefficient enters this chain unless a verified rule says so.
- The contractor's proposed coefficient and price adjustment are out of scope.

**F. On-site materials (ONSITE-01..07, ONSITE-T2-01/02, pp. 235–238).**

- Separate from takeoff, ordinary BOQ pricing and the ordinary price book. Used only in interim statements (ONSITE-01).
- Material price: the Table 2 row price (§11.5). If no Table 2 row exists: chapter row unit price × Table 1 coefficient (§11.3), using the
  lowest-priced row where several apply (ONSITE-04).
- Materials outside both tables become star rows at the end of the relevant Table 2 group (ONSITE-04). Their pricing →
  EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY (star-row instruction).
- Table 2 prices include loading, transport ≤ 30 km and unloading (ONSITE-03). Beyond 30 km, see J.
- Interim payment (ONSITE-05):
  - `material_payment = 70% × applicable on-site-material price`;
  - for materials subject to extra transport cost, the transport cost is included "without applying
    coefficient 0.7": `extra_transport_payment = applicable extra transport cost`, not multiplied by 0.7;
  - this affects only the transport cost covered by that clause; it does not make other transport costs payable and is not
    an ordinary material-pricing rule;
  - regional, overhead and contractor's proposed coefficients are applied "as applicable".
- Eligibility (ONSITE-02) must hold; otherwise the material is not paid.
- The last statement after provisional acceptance and the final statement include no on-site materials (ONSITE-07).
- Row 411004 → UNRESOLVED. The engine never assigns a price to it.

**G. Site setup/removal (SITE-01/02, NEW-04, pp. 2, 246–248, 254).**

- Cap:
  - ابنیه: `cap = 4% × estimate excluding site setup/removal`;
  - several price lists: `cap = Σ(percentage_i × estimate_i excluding site setup)`.
- The capped lump sum excludes rows 990104, 990301–990303 and 991001–991104.
- The cap is a maximum and never an automatic charge. The site setup amount is an input, validated against the cap.
- Exceeding the cap → requires High Technical Council approval before award (flag).
- A change in contract amount does not change the lump sum. The only exception is extra setup for new works, capped at
  25% of the lump sum (NEW-04).
- Payment schedule (SITE-02: 70/30, 30/70, 15/85, then by progress; removal after completion) belongs to contract execution
  and is out of V1 scope.

**H. Star items (STAR-01..03, pp. 1–2).**

- `ratio = Σ starRowEstimate / Σ(base + non-base rowEstimate)`, excluding site setup/removal, per discipline.
- Cap by tender type: public 30%, limited 15%, waived formalities 10%.
- Denominator = 0 → `ERROR`. Exactly at the cap is not "exceeds".
- `ratio > cap` → approval required before tender: all star rows of the discipline go through the executive body → High Technical
  Council secretariat → High Technical Council.
- Star-row pricing method and approval procedure detail → EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY (instruction not found).

**I. New works (NEW-01..04, p254).**

- Existing unit price or method: reuse it with the contract coefficients (NEW-02).
- No existing price: Article 29(c) → EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY.
- `new-work rows + Art. 29(a) increases ≤ 25% × initial contract amount` → validation only. Article 29(a) → EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY.
- Equipment-only approved new work: overhead 1.14 only (NEW-03).
- Extra site setup ≤ 25% × lump-sum site setup (NEW-04).
- Article 29 dependency (Phase 3.5-AB; p254 rechecked visually):
  - Appendix 6 establishes on its own: clause 1 → «بند ج ماده ۲۹ شرایط عمومی پیمان» for new prices; clause 2 reuse with contract
    coefficients and the 25% cap on new-work rows together with Art. 29(a) quantity increases; Note 1 (1.14 only); Note 2 (25%
    of the lump-sum site setup).
  - The PDF names only «شرایط عمومی پیمان» with no title edition, number, date or issuer, and reproduces no Article 29 text.
    No copy exists in the project. The applicable version cannot be identified, so no web search was made.
  - Article 29(a) and 29(c): EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY / UNRESOLVED (document version not identified). Their content is not recorded
    and never becomes a 1404 rule; new-work pricing under 29(c) is not computed, and the 25% check needs the 29(a) increases as
    an explicit input.

**J. Chapter 28 transport (TRANSPORT-01..04, TRANSPORT-QTY-01, pp. 228–232).**

- Only cement, stone materials, steel, brick and block are paid beyond 30 km. Other materials → no separate transport (TRANSPORT-01).
- The distance d is an input: the shortest passable route per the consulting engineer. The supply place must be approved by the employer.
  Missing d or missing approval → `ERROR`; the engine never derives d from an origin.
- Road transport:
  - `amount = tonnage × (d − 30) × rate(band(d))`, where band(d) is chosen by the total supply-to-site distance (§11.6);
  - d ≤ 30 → no Chapter 28 amount.
- Fabricated steel parts from the factory: the related row without deducting 30 km, × 2 (TRANSPORT-03).
- Coefficients:
  - 1.30 for dirt or gravel roads;
  - 1.15 for bulk cement;
  - 1.20 for cold-rolled galvanised steel;
  - 2 for fabricated steel parts from the factory.
- How several of these coefficients combine on the same haul is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK → UNRESOLVED (Phase 3.5-Z;
  pp. 228–232 checked; no other page states a transport-coefficient rule). Each value and scope is verified separately:
  - 1.30: General Requirements clause 4 (p228), transport on dirt or gravel roads; "the rows of this chapter", no route portion
    stated;
  - 1.15: Group 1 clause 1-2-2 (p228), bulk cement, applied to the related rows for the whole route length after deducting 30 km;
  - 1.20: clause 1-4-3 (p230), cold-rolled galvanised steel materials and parts, "the rows of this chapter as applicable";
  - 2: clause 1-4-5 (p230), loading/transport/unloading of factory-fabricated steel parts from the factory, related row without
    the 30 km deduction.
- The source gives no multiplication, sequential order, priority, selection, exclusion or split by cost component, and names no
  external source. Individual verification does not establish a combination rule. None is assumed; a haul where more than one
  coefficient applies gets no computed amount (`NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK`).
- Sea transport:
  - the unit is تن - مایل دریایی;
  - the bands are printed in the row descriptions (pp. 231–232, rechecked visually in Phase 3.5-Y): 280501 تا ۱۰ مایل دریایی;
    280502 مازاد بر ۱۰ تا ۳۰; 280503 مازاد بر ۳۰ تا ۶۰; 280504 مازاد بر ۶۰ تا ۹۰; 280505 مازاد بر ۹۰ تا ۱۵۰ مایل دریایی. They are
    nautical-mile bands of their own; the road km bands are never applied to sea transport;
  - how a distance is priced against these bands is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK → UNRESOLVED. The road rows say "for the
    whole route length after deducting 30 km"; the sea rows and Group 5 clause 5-1 contain no equivalent. Whether each band's
    miles are priced at that band's row (incremental) or the whole distance at one row is not stated, so the engine selects no
    sea row and computes no sea amount (`NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK`);
  - distances over 150 nautical miles have no row → no amount is computed.
- Tonnage = the §11.7 quantity × work quantity, or the cement content formula (input) + 6%.
- TRANSPORT-QTY-01 values are for transport-cost calculation only (clause 5). They are never takeoff, consumption, BOQ or wastage
  coefficients.
- The engine never maps Appendix 1 Table 2 rows to these categories (NO_EXPLICIT_APPENDIX_1_MAPPING).

**K. Chapter 7 rebar (REBAR-01, p56).**

- The weight is calculated from dimensions × table unit weight.
- If the weighed unit weight differs, the weighed value is used, provided `weighed ≤ theoretical + max standard-table tolerance`.
- The tolerance value → UNRESOLVED (Phase 3.5-W). p56 clause 2 prints no number and does not identify which "standard tables"
  (جدول‌های استاندارد) are meant; the weight basis may be standard tables or manufacturer tables. No other page of the 1404 PDF
  gives the value or its source. Clause 1 cites national standard 3132 only for rebar requirements; the source does not link 3132
  to this tolerance, so no tolerance may be imported from 3132 (or any other standard). The engine must not invent it.
- Phase 3.5-AE: p56 clause 2 wording unchanged: theoretical weight «به مأخذ جدول‌های استاندارد یا جدول‌های کارخانه سازنده»;
  weighed unit weight governs «به شرطی که از وزن واحد محاسبه شده تئوری با لحاظ نمودن حداکثر رواداری جدول‌های استاندارد، بیشتر
  نباشد». The tolerance is tied to "standard tables" only; manufacturer tables are a weight basis, not a tolerance source, and
  create no universal tolerance. No official copy of national standard 3132 was obtained (only third-party pages, not used), so
  3132 is neither established nor excluded as the source; it is not linked by the 1404 text. A symmetric (plus-or-minus) value from any
  source is never turned into a + allowance by the engine. No numerical tolerance is authorised by the current verified source
  set; status stays UNRESOLVED, weighed-weight branch `INCOMPLETE`.
- Without the tolerance, the weighed-weight branch cannot be validated → `INCOMPLETE`. No tolerance is imported from any standard
  by the engine, and no wastage or fabrication allowance is added.

**L. Chapter 8 voids (CONC-02, p61).**

- Each void ≤ 0.05 m³ is not deducted from concrete volume. The threshold applies per individual void, never to the total of
  several voids.
- A void of exactly 0.05 m³ is not deducted ("۰/۰۵ مترمکعب یا کمتر").
- Voids > 0.05 m³ → UNRESOLVED (NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_FROM_CURRENT_SOURCE). Phase 3.5-X: p61 rechecked visually; clause 6 is the
  only Chapter 8 void rule, and no other Chapter 8 clause, General Rule or named external source sets the treatment. Similar
  hole/opening rules elsewhere do not apply to Chapter 8 concrete volume: masonry (pp. 35, 85), formwork area (pp. 40, 50), joist
  and block slab area (p80), 3D and other panels (pp. 121, 123), finishes and doors (pp. 132–153). No full, partial,
  proportional or excess-only deduction, and no non-deduction, is assumed; the concrete volume for such an item stays
  `INCOMPLETE`.
- Phase 3.5-AF: p61 clause 6 visually rechecked again, wording unchanged. A full-PDF search for void, hole, empty-space and
  deduction wording found no other clause that governs Chapter 8 concrete volume, no Chapter 8 cross-reference and no named
  external source, so no web search was made. The 1404 source establishes non-deduction for individual voids at or below 0.05
  m³ but does not establish the treatment of individual voids greater than 0.05 m³. No production deduction rule may be created
  from inference: the engine must not deduct the whole void, the excess only or a proportion, must not assume a maximum
  allowance, must not combine several voids into one threshold test and must not silently ignore larger voids.

**M. Clause 10 fittings (GENERAL-10-01, p4).**

- Applies only to fittings (یراق‌آلات) in the light steel, aluminium, wooden, plastic/polymer and glass chapters.
- `amount = employer-approved invoice (supply + transport) × overhead coefficient` (1.14 via OVERHEAD-02 for ابنیه).
- No unit-price adjustment and no other contract coefficient applies.
- Not applicable to ordinary work items.

**N. Chapter 8 concrete measurement basis (Phase 3.5-AG, pp. 4 and 61–66).**

- General Requirements clause 14, printed p4 (visually checked): «اندازه‌گیری کارها، بر اساس ابعاد کارهای انجام شده، که طبق
  ابعاد درج شده در نقشه‌های اجرایی و دستور کارها است، با توجه به مفاد کلیات و الزامات فصل‌ها، صورت می‌گیرد. در مواردی که روش
  ویژه‌ای برای اندازه‌گیری در این فهرست‌بها پیش‌بینی شده است، اندازه‌گیری به روش تعیین شده انجام می‌شود.» Status:
  VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY. Quantities are the dimensions of work done that conform to the dimensions in the execution drawings and
  work instructions; a chapter-specific method takes precedence. The source ties both conditions together, so the engine
  must use neither drawing dimensions alone nor executed dimensions alone as the measured quantity.
- Clause 15, p4: work that will be covered must be recorded in minutes with the consultant before it is covered. This is a
  procedure; it sets no quantity formula.
- Chapter 8 (pp. 61–66): the only general concrete-volume rule is clause 6 (voids, block L). Item-level measurement wording
  exists only for shotcrete on soil walls (Group 7, p63: area measured from the drawings along the executable line), which is
  not a general concrete-volume rule. The flooring 15 cm wording (p62) is an add-on price condition, not a measurement basis.
- NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: concrete placed beyond the drawing dimensions (thicker, wider, deeper or outside the
  geometry), excess caused by the contractor's method, construction tolerances, irregular geometry, approval of executed
  dimensions and revised or as-built drawings. The engine does not pay or reject such excess, applies no tolerance and
  converts neither between drawing and executed dimensions; items that depend on them stay `INCOMPLETE`. No rule from other
  chapters (masonry, excavation, formwork, finishes, slabs, rebar) is imported. No external document is named, so no web
  search was made.
- Note: §10 row F7 cites this clause as printed page 5, but it appears on printed page 4. F1–F9 wording is protected, so the
  row is left unchanged and the discrepancy is only recorded here.

**O. Chapter 8 item-specific measurement (Phase 3.5-AH, pp. 61–66, rendered and checked visually).**

Hierarchy: an explicit Chapter 8 rule applies first; otherwise clause 14 (block N); then an explicitly named external document;
otherwise unresolved. A unit such as m³ or m² is not itself a special rule. All 35 Chapter 8 rows (pp. 64–66) are listed:

- Shotcrete (080701, 080702): unit m² per 1 cm of thickness. Clause 7-1: quantities are the area measured from the drawings
  along the executable line of shotcrete in the soil-wall section. Clause 7-2: the thickness means the minimum sprayed
  thickness, which must nowhere be less than the specification; extra shotcrete to fill surface irregularities and to
  achieve the minimum thickness, and rebound and cleanup, are included in the price. Status VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY. How
  thicknesses above the specified minimum are counted is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.
- Flooring: Chapter 8 has no concrete flooring item. Clause 3-3 only makes add-on 080304 applicable at 15 cm or less and
  gives no extra above that. 080312/080313 are hardening mortar on floor concrete by area. No area, thickness or average
  thickness method for floor concrete is printed, and none is transferred to structural concrete.
- Voids: block L is unchanged; no row above creates a deduction rule.
- No external measurement document is named: standards 18417 and 2930 (Group 2 and Group 8) are material standards only, and
  the star-row instruction (clauses 1-5 and 8-2) is a pricing dependency. No web search was made. Nothing is imported from
  other chapters.
- Engine: every row not listed as special uses clause 14 only; no formula is created for unresolved cases, which stay
  `INCOMPLETE`.

Each rule governs only the row(s) named in its own row text or clause:

| 080311 | row text and clause 3-6 (p62)                                             | add-on for vibration of heavily reinforced concrete                   | m³ of only that volume whose rebar is more than 180 kg per m³          | SUPPLEMENTS_CLAUSE_14                                                   | exactly 180 kg per m³ is not granted by the words more than, and clause 3-6 excludes only less than 180, so it is NOT_SPECIFIED; how that volume is delimited is not specified |

**O.2 Boundary-case recheck (Phase 3.5-AJ).** Whole PDF searched by text layer; candidate pages rendered and viewed (pp.
36, 163; Chapter 8 pp. 62–66 as viewed in Phase 3.5-AH). No case is resolved; all six stay UNRESOLVED and the affected items
stay `INCOMPLETE` (NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK):

1. 080305 keying: «کرم‌بندی» is not defined anywhere. It appears elsewhere only as a cost item under floor paving, Chapter 4
   clause 4-2 (p36) and Chapter 20 clause 12-1 (p163), neither of which refers to Chapter 8. No rule for non-keyed concrete.
   The engine never defines keying from outside knowledge.
2. 080311: only «بیش از ۱۸۰» (row) and «کمتر از ۱۸۰» (clause 3-6) are printed. Exactly 180 kg per m³ and the scope used to
   delimit the qualifying volume (element, pour or other) are not specified. The engine does not pick an equality rule.
3. 080315: no specified-versus-executed thickness rule and no rounding of part-centimetres. The engine chooses no rounding.
4. 080401: no list of rows and no formula; clause 4-1 only excludes strength-priced concrete. The engine creates no
   cement-difference formula.
5. 080602: per-litre excess over 30 litres per metre of anchor; per-anchor versus aggregate is not specified. The engine never
   aggregates grout allowances across anchors.
6. 080701/080702: clause 7-2 makes the minimum thickness a construction requirement; no clause states it is the paid
   thickness or states area times thickness, and thickness above the minimum is not addressed. The engine never treats the
   minimum as automatically payable.

No cross-reference and no external source is named for any case; no web search was made.

General measurement and payment rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY, p67 unless stated):

- Clause 4 (weight rows): weight from drawing and instruction dimensions using standard tables or manufacturer tables. If the
  weighed unit weight differs, it governs provided it is not more than the theoretical unit weight per the manufacturer tables
  «با لحاظ نمودن حداکثر رواداری». No tolerance value is printed: UNRESOLVED. This wording names manufacturer tables, unlike
  Chapter 7 clause 2 (block K); neither clause is used to read the other.
- Clause 5: weight of flame-cut or drilled parts is taken without deducting the cut or hole (also clause 11-1 for 091101,
  091104, 091105). Clause 16-1: for 091601 only the consumed profiles are the weight basis.
- Clause 2: site fabrication approved by the consultant and employer deducts 3 percent from the prices of the Chapter 9 rows.
  Clause 2-1: fabricated parts delivered but not erected are paid at 80 percent of the relevant rows, with consultant
  approval. Clause 11: non-removable anchor or auxiliary parts get no price, except 70 percent of row 090106 where removal is
  impossible for lack of access, with consultant approval (p68).
- Clause 6: preparation, cutting, welding, grinding and electrodes are included; no add-on and no extra weight is paid.
  Clause 7: splicing short profiles is paid only if the specification provides for it; otherwise nothing is paid for the
  weld material weight.
- Clause 8: a connection not included in either member's row is paid separately, welded by weld length from 090801–090805
  and bolted from 091001–091003. Clause 9: where an included welded connection is made with bolts, the bolt weight is paid
  separately from 091001–091003.
- Clause 12-5: space-frame connection welding from 090801–090805; no cost for welding a splice within one member.

Thresholds and boundaries (row text or clause, as printed):

- Welding throat bands 090801–090804: up to 5 mm; more than 5 up to 7; more than 7 up to 10; more than 10 up to 15. Throats
  above 15 mm: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.
- Clause 3-2, 090308–090310 span above 20 m: more than 20 up to 30 m 5 percent, more than 30 up to 40 m 10 percent, more than
  40 up to 50 m twelve and a half percent, added to the row price and applied to the whole span. Spans above 50 m:
  NOT_SPECIFIED.
- Clause 4-2, 090401 span above 30 m: more than 30 up to 40 m 5 percent, more than 40 up to 50 m seven and a half percent,
  applied to the whole span. Spans above 50 m: NOT_SPECIFIED.
- Node weight bands 091101 up to 250 g, 091104 more than 250 g up to 1 kg, 091105 more than 1 kg. Clauses 11-2 and 11-3:
  up to 9 holes (091110) and more than 9 holes (091111).
- 091605: profile or pipe weight less than 2 kg; exactly 2 kg is not covered (UNRESOLVED). 091609: wall thickness more than
  7 mm; exactly 7 mm is not covered (UNRESOLVED); clause 16-2 excludes 091609 where the thickness comes from combining
  profiles. Clause 28-2: free span more than 70 m goes to the star-row instruction.
- 090706: height up to 4 m; above 4 m NOT_SPECIFIED.

Exclusivity and add-ons: clause 11-5, 091110 and 091111 not both for one piece; clause 16-3, 091610 and 091611 not both for
one member; clause 6-1, 090601/090602 in addition to the beam row; 090805 is a percentage add-on to 090801–090804; 092801–
092804 are percentages of the preparation cost; 091606 is a deduction. No add-on applies outside the rows it names.

Cross-references, recorded only: Chapter 7 rows 070612, 070615, 070616 (clause 1-2 base connections); rows 160201, 160230,
160231 (clauses 2-5, 5-2); Chapter 25 painting (clauses 5-2, 12-7); star-row instruction (clauses 3-5, 12-3, 12-6, 12-7,
28-2), EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. Publication 400 «آیین‌نامه سازه‌های فضاکار» (clause 12-1) gives definitions only; national and ISO
bolt standards (clause 10-2) and DIN EN steel naming (clause 12-8) are material references, not measurement sources.

Unresolved (engine stays `INCOMPLETE`, NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK): weighed-weight tolerance; the boundary cases above;
no-price rows 090320, 090321, 090607, 091607, 091611 (never priced); the unit of 091608 and 091609 is not legible; no
rounding or part-unit rule anywhere in Chapter 9. The engine applies no default tolerance, rounding, equality rule,
aggregation or coefficient.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY, p80):

- Clause 1: slabs must meet Publication 543 «دستورالعمل طراحی و اجرای سقف‌های تیرچه و بلوک» (Plan and Budget
  Organization); a requirements document, not a measurement source.
- Clause 3: the rows include supplying and placing joists and blocks, levelling, shoring, formwork, concreting, curing and
  stripping, at any height.
- Clause 4: rebar is paid separately from Chapter 7 rows; open-web steel joists from row 090237.
- Clause 5: on masonry bearing walls or steel beams, the measured area is the whole executed slab, bounded by the outer face
  of the slab edge concrete and edge formwork; no extra for tie beams on bearing walls of slab thickness, and no extra for middle tying members («کلاف‌های دوزنده میانی»); both exclusions are limited to Chapter 10 clause 5.
- Clause 6: on concrete beams or walls, the boundary is the inner face of the beam or wall; the beam and wall concrete and
  formwork are paid from their own rows; no add-on.
- Clause 7: formwork for holes for pipes, service ducts and similar is included; holes smaller than one square metre are not
  deducted from the slab area. Holes of one square metre or more: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK (no deduction method is
  assumed); exactly one square metre is not covered by less than.
- Clause 8: design strength of slab and joist concrete (values as printed, not a measurement rule). Clause 10: block cement
  content (as printed).
- Clause 9: slab thickness is the vertical distance from the joist bottom to the top of the 5 cm concrete over the block.
  Where the specification calls for more than 5 cm, the extra concrete is paid from rows 080105 to 080107 as applicable.
  Exactly 5 cm is the base; the quantity method of the extra concrete follows those rows only as the clause says.
- Clause 11: EPS minimum density 12 kg per m³; each extra kg per m³ required by the specification adds 2 percent to the
  relevant slab rows. Part-kilograms: NOT_SPECIFIED.
- Clause 12: sloping slab, 15 percent added to the slab rows. Clause 13: double joists, 15 percent added to the slab rows.
  Whether 11, 12 and 13 combine by addition or multiplication: NOT_SPECIFIED; the engine chooses none.

Boundaries: 100701 and 100702 include their upper values (حداکثر) and exclude their lower values (بیش از); a strength of
exactly 20 MPa takes no add-on; above 30 MPa: NOT_SPECIFIED. No thickness row exists between or beyond the printed
thicknesses; other thicknesses are NOT_SPECIFIED. No rounding or part-unit rule in Chapter 10.

Isolation: clauses 11–13 apply to the slab rows of this chapter only; 100703 only to concrete-block slab rows; clause 7 hole
rule is a slab-area rule and does not apply to Chapter 8 concrete volume (block L). Cross-references recorded only: Chapter
7 rows, row 090237, rows 080105–080107, Publication 543. Unresolved items stay `INCOMPLETE`; no default rounding,
combination, deduction or price is applied.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY, pp. 85–87):

- Clause 5: holes or openings with a section area smaller than five hundredths of a square metre made during wall building
  get no separate cost and are not deducted. Exactly that area and larger openings: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK. Openings
  made to a design in facing work are priced by the star-row instruction.
- Clause 17: facing is measured on the executed surface; joint area is not deducted; pointing is paid from Chapter 18 rows.
- Clause 8-3: for 110801 to 110817 the whole brickwork volume is measured from the relevant rows, then the add-on is added
  for the faced surfaces. Clause 8-1: 110801 only where the whole wall thickness is one kind of brick. Clause 8-2: facing on
  two faces of a wall one and a half bricks or more takes 110801 for each face. Clause 8-4: 110820 only on the volume of
  red face-brick walling. Clause 8-6: well brickwork from the wall rows plus 110826.
- Clause 6: grout behind brickwork is included. Clause 10: separating facing and backing, facing is paid from the facing rows
  and the rebar ties from row 090701; mortar between is included. Clause 12: steel parts built into brickwork are paid
  separately. Clause 8-7: cavity-wall ties from 090701 and insulation from Chapter 14 rows, both separate. Clause 8-8: rebar
  in 110827 from rows 070302 or 070303. Clause 9-1: lime-soil rows include all placing costs. Clause 5-1: no add-on for
  coloured bricks in group 5.
- Coefficients (values in words): clause 13, arched walls of one brick or less, 30 percent on the arched surface; clause 16,
  rows 110830 to 110832 apply with coefficient one and three tenths to hollow brick walls and one quarter to clay block walls;
  clause 12-2, refractory brick of colours other than white or black at coefficient one and four tenths. Clause 11: tile and
  refractory brick facing with each face smaller than two and a half hundredths of a square metre and face at most 350 mm;
  otherwise star-row pricing.
- Clause 14: extra cement is paid from row 080401. Clauses 15 and 11-2: mortar thicker than the average (3 cm; two and a half cm for floor paving) is paid at 50 percent of rows 180304 to 180306 or 180308 to 180310, by consultant
  instruction.
- Clause 2 defines brick dimensions and wall thicknesses (definitions only).

Boundaries: band wording as printed. Clay block 7 to 11 cm, more than 11 up to 22, more than 22; refractory 20 to 30, more
than 30 up to 40, more than 40 up to 50 mm, above 50 NOT_SPECIFIED; 111102 above 70 mm NOT_SPECIFIED; 111001 up to 10 cm²
and 111002 more than 10 cm²; 111101 up to 7 dm², above that NOT_SPECIFIED. Only 110904, 110905 and 111102 give a part-unit
rule (pro rata); no other rounding rule exists.

External references (material only, not measurement sources): national standards 7 (clause 1), 22367 (1-1), 7122 (4-1),
19481 (11-1); star-row instruction (clauses 5, 11, 12-1): EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY.

Isolation: the clause 5 hole rule is for Chapter 11 brickwork only and is not the Chapter 8 void rule (block L) or the Chapter
10 slab-hole rule (block Q); clause 16 coefficients apply only to 110830–110832; 110820 only to its listed rows; the
pro-rata rules only to 110904, 110905 and 111102.

Unresolved (`INCOMPLETE`): openings of five hundredths of a square metre or more; thicknesses and sizes outside the printed
bands; price cells of 110830 and 111102 read one (not used as prices); how clause 13, 16 and 12-2 coefficients combine with
other add-ons. The engine applies no default rounding, combination, deduction or price.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY):

- Clause 1: formwork, setting mortar and pointing are included, except mastic, where no separate row exists. Clause 2: steel
  parts are paid from the relevant chapter rows. Clause 3: difficulty of reinforced concrete is included. Clause 4: extra
  cement from row 080401.
- Clause 1-2: kerb section means the section perpendicular to the route. Clause 1-4: kerbs measured by their own volume
  without setting mortar. Clause 1-6: formwork and setting included; lean concrete, levelling concrete and backing concrete
  paid from their rows; formwork of backing concrete, if the specification calls for it and the consultant approves, from row
  050102 or 060102. Clause 1-3: canopy supply from the kerb rows.
- Clause 2-1: straps, connections, geotextile and backfill for 120203 and 120204 paid separately. Clause 2-2: measurement
  basis for 120203 to 120205 is the concrete volume of the pieces; no add-on for any shape. Clause 2-4: panel caps from row 120204.
- Clause 4-2: filling behind well rings (average up to 10 cm) is included in 120401 and 120402.
- Clause 8-2: galvanised ties in 120801 to 120807 are included; polymer foam at joints from Chapter 14. Clause 8-3: panels
  120820 to 120824, minimum area half a square metre up to one square metre; connection parts included; holding frames from
  Chapter 16; panels larger than one square metre by specification take the relevant row at coefficient one and fifteen
  hundredths. Clause 11-1: rebar in 121101 from 070302 or 070303. Clause 11-2: 121101 does not apply to AAC block walls.
- Coefficients (values in words): clauses 3-1 and 4-3, one and seven hundredths where pipe or ring laying needs pumping;
  clause 5-7, one and two tenths where lightweight-block mortar density is below 1000 kg per m³ after drying; clause 8-1,
  nine tenths for non-autoclaved AAC blocks; clause 12-1, one and one tenth for C250 and D400 covers.
- Clause 12-2: laying mortar and rebar included in 121201; mortar is cement-sand 1:5 unless specified.

Boundaries (as printed): kerbs 120101 up to five hundredths of a square metre section, 120102 more than five hundredths up
to one tenth, 120103 more than one tenth; pressed kerbs 120104 up to six hundredths, 120105 more than six hundredths. Pipe
diameters and wall thicknesses as printed per row; other sizes NOT_SPECIFIED. 120403 above 120 cm NOT_SPECIFIED. Clause 8-3
panels below half a square metre NOT_SPECIFIED; exactly one square metre falls under up to one square metre. Clause 5-7
density exactly 1000 kg per m³ is not covered by below. Only 120403 has a part-unit rule; no other rounding rule.

External references: star-row instruction (clauses 6, 1-7): EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. Publications 101 and 308 (clause 2-3) and
national standards 12728, 16494, 70, 7782, 7657, 8593, 14504, 706 and 14976: material or technical references only, not
measurement sources.

Isolation: clause 1-4 and 2-2 volume bases apply only to their rows; coefficients apply only to their named rows or groups;
121101 is excluded from AAC walls; add-ons 120524, 120534, 120543, 121004 apply only to their own ranges; nothing is taken
from Chapter 11 brickwork (block R).

price); sizes outside printed bands; exact boundary cases above; how coefficients combine with add-ons. The engine applies

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY):

- Clause 2: the measurement basis is the visible surface of damp-proofed work, per the dimensions in the specification and
  measurement minutes (صورت‌جلسه‌ها); no separate cost for overlap. This chapter-wide rule applies to Chapter 13 only.
- Clause 1: overlap per the specification; 10 cm where not set. Overlap is not measured or paid separately.
- Clause 3: prices apply to all surfaces (horizontal, vertical, sloped, curved and similar); no add-on for difficulty, depth,
  curvature or similar, unless this chapter states otherwise. Clause 4 and footnote 41: other surfaces means surfaces other
  than bathrooms, toilets and footings. Clause 5: bitumen means solid bitumen (pure or oxidised) for any climate unless
  stated. Clause 6: substrate preparation needs supervisor approval before damp-proofing.
- Clause 1-3: where the specification sets bitumen per m² other than one kg for 130102, 130108, 130109, the row prices are
  adjusted pro rata for the decrease or increase, up to a maximum of 2 kg per m². Clause 2-1: bitumen per m² for group 2 is
  printed per layer count (values in the source, written here as three and a half, 5, and six and a half kg).
- Clause 3-2: primer in 130303 to 130308 is cutback bitumen at at least 280 g per m² and is included in their price. Clause
  3-3: where a bitumen coat replaces the primer, the deduction of row 130314 applies and row 130101 is paid for the bitumen
  coat.
- Clauses 4-1 to 4-3: 130403 dry film thickness one half mm plus or minus five hundredths, pro rata up to one mm plus or
  minus five hundredths; 130404 one and a half mm plus or minus one tenth, pro rata up to 3 mm plus or minus one tenth; 130405
  one kg powder per m², pro rata up to 3 kg per m²; each only where the specification requires more. Clause 5-2: 130501 pro
  rata where the specified thickness is greater or smaller than printed.
- Clause 4-4 defines negative water pressure for 130405 (definition only).

Boundaries: pro-rata adjustments stop at the printed maxima (clause 1-3 two kg; clause 4-1 one mm; clause 4-2 three mm;
clause 4-3 three kg); beyond them NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK. Clause 1-3 decreases have no lower limit printed; clauses
4-1 to 4-3 cover increases only, so thinner layers are NOT_SPECIFIED. Clause 5-2 has no limit printed. How the printed
tolerance band enters the pro-rata calculation is NOT_SPECIFIED. No rounding rule.

External references: national standards 3885, 3864, 3884, 16951, 1333; ASTM C836, BS EN 14891, BS EN 1504-2: material and
technical references only, not measurement sources. Technical-specification quantities (clauses 1, 1-3, 4-1 to 5-2) are
project inputs; without them the adjustment stays `INCOMPLETE`.

Isolation: clause 2 visible-surface and overlap rule applies only to Chapter 13; pro-rata rules only to their named rows;

Chapter 12 or 14.
