Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY):

- Clause 1: except fire-resistant coatings, the measurement basis is the visible insulated surface per the dimensions in the
  specification and measurement minutes. Clause 7-3: for fire-resistant coatings the basis is the work surface per drawing
  dimensions and minutes of the coated surfaces; for castellated beams the hole area is counted on one side and deducted on
  the other; trapezoidal voids between steel beams and permanent metal decking are counted from the coating rows, and
  filling them with consultant-approved material gets no separate cost.
- Clause 2: substrate preparation for thermal insulation is paid from the relevant rows. Clause 5-1: preparation, drilling
  and fixings are included in 140511. Clause 7-6: primer, welded mesh, lath, galvanised mesh, steel pins and similar get no
  separate cost except under clause 7-7; clause 7-7: where the specification or geometry requires mesh with consultant
  approval, it is paid from Chapter 16 rows.
- Clause 1-3: extra insulation weight from greater thickness or density is paid by the add-on row, in addition to the base
  row, for the excess over the base values. The worked example (p109) computes the extra kg per m² as the difference of
  thickness times density between specified and base, then multiplies by the area. This formula is VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY for the
  group 1 add-ons; the base row pays the area. Its use for groups 2 and 6 add-ons, which share the row text but not clause
  1-3, is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.
- Clause 7-4: where the specification sets resistance above 60 minutes, add 25 percent per extra 30 minutes for rows with
  section factor up to 90 m per m², and 35 percent for rows with section factor above 90 m per m². The worked example (p112)
  divides the extra minutes by 30 and multiplies by the percentage (fractional result, 140 percent for 180 minutes). Clause
  7-5: hollow sections take coefficient one and five hundredths for section factor up to 90 and one and fifteen hundredths
  above 90. The p111 example computes the section factor as the heated perimeter divided by the section area, and the area as
  heated perimeter times length plus the trapezoid voids on both sides.
- Clause 3: polymer foams must meet at least class E of national standard 8299-1 (material requirement).

Boundaries: section-factor bands 140711 and 140714 up to 90, 140712 and 140715 more than 90 up to 200, 140713 and 140716
more than 200 m per m². Exactly 90 falls under up to 90 (clause 7-4 also says up to 90). Resistance up to 60 minutes is the
base; the p112 example shows the 30-minute steps applied pro rata. Critical temperatures other than 550 and 620 degrees:
NOT_SPECIFIED. Thickness or density below the base values: no deduction is printed, NOT_SPECIFIED. Part-kilograms pro rata
only in the add-on rows that say so.

Price cells: 140807 has no price (never priced). 140717 and 140718 are negative percentages (minus 5 and minus 10) and are
deductions, not prices. No cell reads one in this chapter.

External references (material and technical only): national standards 8299-1, 8116, 8084, 10950, 10952, 17197-1, 11108,
11056; ASTM C1427, ASTM C1029; BS EN 13381 (section factor definition); Publication 714 (group 8); certification by the Road,
Housing and Urban Development Research Center (group 8 note). Fire test certificates (clause 7-1) are project evidence.

Isolation: the clause 1 visible-surface rule applies to thermal insulation only, clause 7-3 to fire-resistant coatings only;
the Chapter 13 overlap rule (block T) is not imported; clause 7-4 and 7-5 percentages and coefficients apply only to
140711–140716; 140717 and 140718 only to their named rows; Chapter 14 rules are not applied to Chapters 13 or 15.

`INCOMPLETE`: 140807 price; add-on formula outside group 1; below-base thickness or density; other critical temperatures;
how clause 7-4, 7-5 and 140717/140718 combine (the source does not state the order); rounding. The engine applies no
default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

- Coefficients and percentages: clause 6-5 adds one and a half percent per kg per m³ of polystyrene density above 15 (160601)
  or 20 (160602); clause 6-20 adds two and a half percent per kg per m³ above 30 (160608) or 20 (160609, 160610); clause
  6-18 adds 5 percent to ceiling rows for slope from 10 to 30 percent, none below 10; clause 6-17 pays stair slabs at 60
  percent of 160608 with the wall-thickness add-ons.
- Approval: 160406 specification by consultant before order (4-2); 160801 conditions from a manufacturer before estimate,
  employer approval, EN 12825, priced by the star-item instruction (8-1); 160901, 160902 specification approved by the
  consultant (9-1).

Thresholds and boundaries: 160230 galvanising up to 60 micron; 160231 per 10 micron above 60, part of 10 pro rata (row
text). 160611 per 5 cm above 20 up to 45 cm; 160614 per cm above 20 up to 32 cm; 160612 height above three and a half up
to five and a half m; 160419 sheet thickness six tenths to 6 mm, apertures 14 by 32 to 100 by 250 mm; 160225 decorative
width at most 50 cm (2-13); 160705 decorative width less than 90 cm (7-5); 160421, 160422 diameters 60 and 90 cm.
Values beyond the printed ranges (wall thickness above 45 or 32 cm, height above five and a half m, slope above 30
percent), partial 5 cm steps in 160611, and densities below the minima: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK; no
interpolation, extrapolation or deduction. Combination order of the density, slope, thickness, height and external-wall
add-ons is not stated: NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.

  grilles and similar, each of area less than a quarter of a square metre, are not deducted. Exactly a quarter of a square
  metre is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.

than 72 dm²; 200611 piece area up to one tenth dm², 200612 more than one tenth to 1 dm²; joint 3 mm or more.
NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: pool tile pieces above 1 dm²; absorption between 3 and 10 percent for 200121 to 200124,
and above 6 percent for 200321 to 200324; whether the relief 6 percent applies to 200511, 200611, 200612; mortar
thickness partial amounts; combination order of the 4, 6, 9 percent add-ons, absorption add-ons, adhesive add-ons and 200803.

Price cells: 200127 and 200327 have no printed price; each is INCOMPLETE, never priced from neighbouring rows, and the
percentage add-ons have no base for them. 200803 is a negative deduction, not a price. No cell reads one.

Source inconsistencies: none confirmed. Every cited range (200121 to 200127, 200321 to 200327, 200611 to 200614, 180304
to 180306, 180308 to 180310) contains only printed codes; no duplicate codes were found.

External references (material and technical only unless stated): national standards 25, 9169, 12492, 12495, 3051, 4289,
10900, 12659, 14499; publication 714. The star-item instruction is EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. Contents are not reproduced here.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 20 rows named; they do not leak to Chapter 16, Chapter 17, Chapter 18,
Chapter 19, Chapter 21 or other chapters. The Chapter 18 plastering and pointing rules and the Chapter 19 wood rules
(blocks Y, Z) are not imported. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or
price.

**AB. Chapter 21 mosaic and concrete paving (Phase 3.5-AW, printed pp. 168–172, every page rendered and viewed).**

Title, from the rendered page: «فصل بیست و یکم. فرش موزاییک و کفپوش بتنی». Five pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی)
clauses 1–12 with sub-clauses (pp. 168–169; clause 12 is the group table, groups 1–8); group requirements for groups 2, 3,
5, 7 and 8 (pp. 169–170); 23 price rows (pp. 171–172). Printed p173 opens «فصل بیست و دوم. کارهای سنگی با سنگ پلاک», so
Chapter 21 ends on p172. Codes and units read from the rendered pages; every row is m²:

| group                                                  | rows                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------ |
| 1 precast concrete pavers on cement mortar             | 210105, 210106, 210107, 210108 |
| 2 precast concrete pavers on soft sand                 | 210205, 210206                 |
| 3 thickness, textured and coloured add-ons for pavers  | 210305, 210306, 210307, 210308 |
| 4 mosaic flooring                                      | 210403, 210404                 |
| 5 machine mosaic flooring                              | 210511, 210512, 210513, 210514 |
| 6 washed mosaic (واش‌بتن)                              | 210601, 210602                 |
| 7 aggregate, marble chip and colour add-ons for mosaic | 210701, 210702, 210703         |
| 8 grinding of mosaic surfaces                          | 210801, 210802                 |

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 210305–210308, 210701–210703, 210802; each applies only to the rows its own text names (210701
and 210703 to 210403 to 210602; 210702 to 210403 and 210404 only; 210802 to 210801).

Clause 14 relation: clause 5 supplements clause 14 with a named basis: measurement is on the executed work surface
(«سطح کار اجرا شده»); joint area is not deducted.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows named:

- Clause 4: mosaic sizes are assumed 30 by 30 or 40 by 40 cm, washed mosaic 30 by 30, 30 by 40, 40 by 40 or 30 by 60 cm;
  other sizes are NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK. Clause 7: laying mortar is cement mortar one to five; grout is cement and
  stone powder one to six.
- Clause 8-1: laying mortar averages two and a half cm; extra mortar used to fill under the floor, by consultant
  instruction, is paid at 50 percent of 180308 to 180310. Clause 2-1: in 210205, 210206 sand averages two and a half cm;
  extra sand by consultant instruction from 040502. Clause 8-2 and 11: screeding, levelling, soil compaction and concrete
  are not included and are paid from their chapters. Clause 9: joint forming without filling from 060911; asphalt joint
  filling from 270503. These rows of Chapters 4, 6, 18 and 27 are cited payment sources only.
- Included: autoclave curing in machine mosaic and precast paver rows (10); factory grinding (8-1); grinding equipment and
  materials (8-2). Clause 8-1: up to two grinding coats after laying, where needed with consultant approval, from 210801;
  more than two coats by consultant instruction from 210802 per extra coat. Clause 3-2: textured pavers take 210307;
  clause 3-3: coloured (non-grey) pavers take 210308. Clause 7-1: unspecified cement is ordinary portland; white or
  coloured cement in body or face takes 210703. Clause 5-1 defines machine mosaic.
- Coefficient: clause 6: where the specification needs a floor slope of more than 20 percent, the related rows take
  coefficient one and six hundredths (multiplier). Exactly 20 percent takes no coefficient («بیش از»).
- Approval: specification, thickness, colour and type approved by the consultant before supply (3, 3-1).

Thresholds and boundaries: pavers 4 cm thick, area up to 16 dm² (210105, 210107, 210205, 210206) or more than 16 dm²
(210106, 210108); 210305, 210306 per cm of thickness above 4 up to 8 cm; 210701 aggregate 20 mm or more; slope more than
20 percent. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: paver thickness above 8 cm and partial centimetres; soft-sand pavers above 16
dm²; which paver rows 210307 and 210308 apply to beyond the words pressed and vibrated; combination order of the slope
coefficient, the add-ons and extra mortar or sand.

Price cells: all 23 rows carry a printed price; no blank cell, no cell reads one, no negative or percentage row.

Source inconsistencies: none confirmed. Cited ranges 210403 to 210602, 210403 to 210404 and 180308 to 180310 contain only
printed codes; no duplicate codes. Asymmetry recorded literally, not reconciled: 210702 covers only 210403 and 210404,
while 210701 and 210703 cover 210403 to 210602; the engine must not extend 210702 to groups 5 and 6.

External references (material and technical only): national standards 755-1, 755-2, 20185. No star-item instruction is
cited in this chapter. Contents are not reproduced here.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 21 rows named; they do not leak to Chapter 18, Chapter 19, Chapter 20,
Chapter 22 or other chapters. The Chapter 20 tiling rules and Chapter 18 plastering rules (blocks AA, Y) are not
imported. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AC. Chapter 22 stone works with slab stone (Phase 3.5-AX, printed pp. 173–187, every page rendered and read glyph by glyph).**

Title, from the rendered page: «فصل بیست و دوم. کارهای سنگی با سنگ پلاک». Fifteen pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی) clauses 1 to 15, with sub-clauses 5-1, 5-2, 5-3 and a printed worked example (مثال), and 14-1, 14-2 (pp. 173–174; clause 15 is the group table, groups 1 to 12 printed 01 to 12); group requirements for all twelve groups, with sub-clauses 6-1 to 6-7, 7-1 to 7-4, 8-1, 8-2, 9-1 to 9-5, 10-1, 11-1 to 11-4, 12-1, 12-2 (pp. 175–177); 144 price rows (pp. 178–187). Printed p188 opens «فصل بیست و سوم. کارهای پلاستیکی و پلیمری», so Chapter 22 ends on p187. Codes and units read from the rendered pages; 134 rows are m² and 10 rows are metres (220611 to 220616, 220710, 220712, 220713, 220809):

| 1 travertine slabs on horizontal surfaces, rubble slabs for floors (220160) | 220110, 220113, 220114, 220115, 220116, 220117, 220118, 220121, 220124, 220125, 220126, 220127, 220128, 220129, 220130, 220131, 220134, 220136, 220139, 220140, 220141, 220142, 220143, 220144, 220145, 220146, 220147, 220148, 220151, 220154, 220155, 220156, 220157, 220160 |

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 220601 to 220620 of group 6, 220710 to 220713, 220802, 220804, 220806 and 220808, 221101, 221102 and 221203; 220925 is a deduction (کسر بها). Each applies only to the rows or conditions its own text names.

Clause 14 relation: clause 8 supplements clause 14 with a named basis: measurement is on the finished exposed work surface («سطح کار تمام‌شده نمایان»); surfaces under window-sill covers and stairs, the stone thickness and similar non-exposed surfaces are not counted; stone executed above false-ceiling level is counted when the specifications require it. Clause 7 classifies inclined surfaces: up to 30 degrees from vertical are vertical and more than 30 degrees are horizontal. Clause 8-1 sets the kerb basis: the price of one row of kerb over the work length, including the net kerb length, measured as the work surface along the alignment and without the joint thickness between kerbs. Clause 9-3: cubical stone is measured on the executed work surface and joint area is not deducted.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 1: the stone sample must reach the consultant for approval against the specifications before supply. Clause 2: the colours are the natural market colours of the stones and the quarry province is named in parentheses.
- Clause 3: the row prices assume slab width up to 40 cm; width more than 40 up to a maximum of 60 cm adds 35 percent of the related rows; width more than 60 cm goes to the star-item instruction.
- Clause 4: where slab dimensions are restricted in both directions, or shapes without right angles are chosen, 10 percent of the related row price is counted separately to compensate the «حکمی» shaping of the stone.
- Clause 5: slab thickness is one and a half to 2 cm. More than 2 up to 3 cm: each half cm added brings 25 percent of the two-cm stone row price. More than 3 up to 4 cm: each half cm added brings 15 percent of the three-cm stone price. More than 4 cm: star-item instruction. The printed example states the order: the three-cm price takes coefficient one and five tenths of the two-cm price, and the four-cm price takes coefficient one and three tenths of the three-cm price, that is one and three tenths by one and five tenths of the two-cm price.
- Clause 6: where slabs are chiselled («تیشه‌ای») or pitch-dressed («کلنگی») so that polishing (ساب) is not needed, 15 percent of the base price of the two-cm stone is deducted from the final stone price and add-on row 220607 applies.
- Clause 9: white or coloured grout materials for stone floors or facades cause no price difference. Clause 10: where the joint between slabs is more than 3 mm, the joint filling is paid separately from 180808.
- Clause 11: fixing mortar for slabs on horizontal surfaces, and grout behind vertical slabs and cornices: sand-cement one to four; joint grout: white cement and stone powder one to four. Clause 12: rubble-slab (لاشه) floor mortar: sand-cement one to five; grout and joint filling: sand-cement and stone powder one to four. Clause 13: kerb and cubical-stone mortar: sand-cement one to three; joint mortar: «ماسه بادی» sand and cement one to four.
- Clause 14: floor mortar averages two and a half cm; extra mortar, by the consultant's work order, is paid at 50 percent of 180308 to 180310. Sub-clause 14-1: rounding (کرم‌بندی) and filling under floors without sand-cement mortar come from the related rows. Sub-clause 14-2: vertical-surface mortar averages 3 cm; extra mortar, by the contract documents, is paid at 50 percent of 180304 to 180306.
- 6-1: 220602 and 220603 include the full provision and installation of «اسکوپ» anchors or stainless-steel wire; 220602 is for slabs other than granite and 220603 for granite, both for vertical surfaces. 6-2 and 6-3: threshold (آستانه) stones take 220605, and when 220605 is counted, 220601 is not counted; 7-1 and 11-4 state the same for the group 7 rows and for 221101 or 221102.
- 6-4: 220610 (flame finishing) applies only to the flamed part of the stone surface. 6-5: chamfering, dowel and groove work at any angle and dimension, and rounding slab edges to any radius, come from 220611 to 220616. 6-6: 220617 includes the drilling, the bolt installation and covering the bolt face after installation.
- 6-7: where adhesive replaces mortar on vertical surfaces, the adhesive sample with its catalogue, specifications and standard must be approved by the consultant and the employer before supply, and 220620 is priced by the star-item instruction.
- 7-2: 220711 applies to the stair floor stone and the stair tread stone («پاخور»، «پیشانی»). 7-3: fetileh («فتیله») is stone with a cross-section of 30 cm² or less, and 220713 applies to cornice or skirting stone. 7-4: in 220710 to 220713, chamfering, edge rounding, grooving and «آبچکان» are paid separately from the related rows.
- 8-2: the kerb rows include joint filling; back concrete, lean concrete under the kerb («زیرجدول») and layer concrete are paid from their related rows; formwork behind kerbs, with consultant approval, comes from 050102 or 060102.
- 9-1: رگلاژ, slope shaping, soil compaction, preparation and lean concrete are not included in the cubical-stone rows and are paid from their related chapters. 9-2: unspecified cubical stone is four cut faces and two guillotine-cut faces («گیوتینی»), or six cut faces; cubes of 10 by 10 by 10 cm add 50 percent of the related rows; irregular cubical stone (all faces guillotine-cut) counts 75 percent of the related rows — the printed word is counted («منظور می‌شود»), not added, and the engine must not read an addition into it. 9-4: cubical-stone floors laid at a slope of more than 20 percent, with consultant approval, take coefficient one and five hundredths.
- 9-5: the sand of 220925 averages two and a half cm; extra sand, by the consultant's work order, comes from 040502.
- 10-1: بادبر slabs are 15 by 30 cm and 2 cm thick without taking «بار سنگ» into account; the clause 4 add-on belongs to 221001 to 221004 only when the two dimensions governed differ from 15 by 30.
- 11-1: 221101 and 221102 must follow publication 714. 11-2: 221101 is concealed dry fixing, with anchors and moulds behind the pieces, each piece adjustable and replaceable anywhere on the facade and not visible in it; 221102 is exposed dry fixing, with visible clips holding the pieces and connected to the substructure and the aluminium grid. 11-3: the steel substructure for dry fixing comes from 160223 and 160224, aluminium profiles from 170117.
- 12-1: the unit prices include factory polishing; polishing after installation, on horizontal surfaces, when needed and approved by the consultant, comes from 221201 to 221203. 12-2: these rows include all equipment, tools and materials for the polishing.
- Kerb thickness: 220802, 220804, 220806 and 220808 pay each extra cm above the 6 cm base up to 10 cm and belong to 220801, 220803, 220805 and 220807 respectively. Polishing stages: 221201 covers up to 7 stages for stone other than granite, 221202 up to 10 stages for granite, and 221203 pays each stage added beyond those, up to 20.

Thresholds and boundaries: slab width up to 40 cm, more than 40 up to 60 cm, more than 60 cm; slab thickness one and a half to 2 cm, more than 2 up to 3 cm, more than 3 up to 4 cm, more than 4 cm; inclination up to 30 degrees from vertical; joint more than 3 mm; fetileh cross-section 30 cm² or less; kerb thickness 6 cm base with extras up to 10 cm; cubical stone 5 by 10 by 10 cm standard and 10 by 10 by 10 cm; polishing up to 7 stages, up to 10 stages and up to 20 stages; mortar two and a half cm on floors, 3 cm on vertical surfaces, two and a half cm of sand under 220925. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: partial half-cm thickness steps inside a band; slab thickness below one and a half cm; kerb thickness above 10 cm; polishing stages above 20; cubical-stone sizes other than the two named; how the 75 percent of 9-2 combines with other adjustments; the combination order of the width 35 percent, the clause 4 10 percent, the clause 5 thickness coefficients, the clause 6 deduction, the group 6 to 8 add-on rows, the 9-2 adjustments and the 9-4 coefficient — only the clause 5 example states its own internal order.

Price cells: 13 rows have no printed price and each is INCOMPLETE, never priced from neighbouring rows: 220124, 220125, 220126, 220129, 220130, 220140, 220141, 220147, 220620, 220803, 220913, 220917, 220918. The cell of 220804 reads one and is INCOMPLETE as a price. 220925 is a negative deduction (کسر بها), not a price. The remaining 130 rows carry printed numeric prices, of which 220925 is the only negative one.

Source inconsistencies: the group 4 requirement (china slabs) cites standard 5696 with the marble title «سنگ ساختمانی - مرمریت - ویژگی‌ها», the same standard and title as group 3 (marble); recorded as printed, and the engine must not invent a separate china standard. The kerb rows 220801 to 220808 print unit m² while clause 8-1 describes the basis as one row of kerb along the work length; recorded as printed, and the engine must not convert units or assume a per-metre basis. The cell of 220804 reads one. No cited range contains a missing code and no duplicate code was found: 221001 to 221004, 220910 to 220918, 220710 to 220713, 221201 to 221203, 180304 to 180306, 180308 to 180310, and 040502, 050102, 060102, 160223, 160224, 170117 and 180808 are all printed rows.

External references (material and technical only unless stated): national standards 13247 (travertine), 5695 (limestone), 5696 (marble, and group 4 china with the marble title) and 5694 (granite); publication 714 for 221101 and 221102 (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: its facade design and execution rules are required); the star-item instruction for slab width above 60 cm, slab thickness above 4 cm and 220620 (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: those prices cannot be formed without it); the adhesive catalogue and standard approval of 6-7. Contents are not reproduced here.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 22 rows named; they do not leak to Chapter 16, Chapter 17, Chapter 18, Chapter 19, Chapter 20, Chapter 21, Chapter 23 or other chapters. The Chapter 4, 5, 6, 16, 17 and 18 rows cited above are payment sources only. The group 9 slope coefficient one and five hundredths is not the Chapter 21 slope coefficient one and six hundredths and the two must not be conflated. The Chapter 20 tiling, Chapter 21 mosaic and Chapter 18 plastering rules (blocks AA, AB, Y) are not imported. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.
