No deduction (کسر بها) row and no negative price is printed in this chapter.

Clause 14 relation: the General Requirements contain no chapter-wide measurement clause; the special bases are group- or row-specific and supplement Clause 14. 6-5 (group 5) and 1-8 (row 230801): the measurement basis is the executed work surface without counting the corrugation or the overlap, and no add-on is paid for difficulty, curvature and the like. 10-5: the metre unit of the skylight bases 230540 and 230541 is measured per the perimeter of the intersection of the base with the substrate. 11-5: the bases and bubbles 230542 to 230544 are measured per the horizontal projection of the useful dimensions of the bubble. 1-2: the thickness of the base-bearing granular flooring 230242 is the vertical distance from the underside of the base to the flooring surface. 5-1: the thickness of flake-pattern (پولکی) flooring excludes the flake relief. 6-1: for the sports-hall rows 230130 to 230132 the P.V.C layer is one and a half to 2 mm and the remainder of the thickness is the foam layer. 4-3: with silica granules the epoxy thickness of 230350 is computed without the silica layer. 6-1-11: the price of 231110 is based solely on the U.P.V.C profile weight without the galvanised reinforcing sheet and the rubber strip, computed from the specifications and work orders per the manufacturer tables, compared with the weighed values, and the lower weight is the basis. 7-1-11: the weight of the profiles consumed in the work is computed and applied.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 1: the flooring installation rows include the substrate preparation materials per the specifications. Clause 2: material specifications, their source of procurement and any cost-relevant information must be stated in the specifications. Clause 3: thickness tolerances of the values stated in this chapter's rows may be up to one tenth of a millimetre, and no deduction or add-on is considered on this account. Clause 4: no payment is made for waste, execution difficulty, fixing tools and devices, plastic joint welding wires and the like; substrate execution is paid from the related rows of the other chapters per the specifications. Clauses 5 and 6: anti-bacterial flooring per standard 10900 and conductive or antistatic flooring per standard 10656 must be controlled and approved.
- 3-1: no add-on is paid for single- or multi-layer, high- or low-traffic, coloured or patterned P.V.C flooring. 4-1: product specifications, product standards and commodity certificates are approved by the consultant before procurement.
- 2-2: the granular rubber rows 230240 and 230242 are priced for the green, ochre, black, pencil-grey, silver and brown colours; other colours per the specifications take coefficient one and one tenth. 3-2 and 4-2: the turf rows 230250 and 230251 follow standards 21124-1 and 21124-2, with a finished height of 45 to 55 mm, self-extinguishing, colour-fast and UV-stable fibres of at least 1200 grams per square metre. 5-2: no add-on is paid for coloured fibres, overlap, waste, creasing or fixing tools; the turf substrate is computed from the other rows; the initial geotextile layer is paid, as applicable, from the rows of group twelve of this chapter (see inconsistencies); the connection strip, the silica spread and the granules, each up to 2 cm, are included in the unit prices. 6-2: the turf testing costs are included and a field test is required after execution. 7-2: turf used away from football fields is paid from 230250 and 230251 with no addition or deduction.
- 1-3: the layered sports rows 230340 and 230341 follow standard 19397. 2-3: all execution costs of 230350 and 230360 are included; no separate payment is made for de-airing, sanding and the like beyond what is explicitly stated. 3-3: the fiberglass mesh reinforcing 230350 and 230360 is paid, with consultant approval, separately from row 130406 with coefficient eighty-five hundredths (see inconsistencies).
- 1-4: «لبه پوشش پلاستیکی» in 230402 means the P.V.C trims of H, F, U and similar shapes, any dimensions, fixed or folding. 2-4: 230402 and 230403 are simple or printed; a covered version takes the add-on 230410. 3-4: the downpipe row 230430 follows standard 12142-1.
- 1-5 to 4-5: 230501 follows standard 1435, 230503 follows 16121, 230505 and 230520 follow 21074 and BS EN 16153, and 230530 follows 16051. 5-5: when the polymer sheet thickness differs from 230501 to 230506, the price is computed pro rata to the increase or decrease of thickness (the interpolation basis is not stated). 7-5: the substrate work of the sheet rows is paid from the related rows. 8-5: 230510 and 230511 include the connection pieces at the sheet joints, such as the H profile and the fish-mouth, with rubber sealing strips. 9-5: UV-resistant extruded polypropylene sheets take coefficient one and one tenth on 230530 and 230531. 12-5: the installation labour of the opening hardware of the bubble skylights is included; the hardware itself is procured per the general provisions of this pricebook.
- 1-6: 230620 follows standard 19086. 2-6: 230620 is the double-wall panel wall covering of 6 to 10 mm with interlocking edges, wall preparation included; 230610 and 230615 are the ceiling tiles or panels of 6 to 10 mm with dry installation. 4-6: 230615 and 230620 are simple or printed without cover; the covered version takes 230625. 5-6: 230630 is the U.P.V.C panel of 20 to 22 cm width and 2 mm thickness with round interlocking edges, the self-drilling screw connection included.
- 1-9 and 2-9: the P.V.C waterstops 230910 to 230914 follow standards 13277-1 and 13277-2; 230910 to 230912 are type D and 230913 and 230914 are type A. 3-9: the joining of the waterstops is included. 4-9: all materials and implements for waterstop installation are included except the consumed rebar, which is paid from row 090701. 5-9 and 6-9: 230920 is the sodium-bentonite waterstop and 230925 the hydrophilic non-vulcanised-rubber waterstop, for the cold and construction joints, tanks, sewers and treatment plants stated. 7-9 and 8-9: the mastics 230930 and 230931 follow standard 17500; 230930 is one-part polyurethane and 230931 one-part silicone.
- 1-10 to 4-10: 231003 follows standard 12027, 231004 and 231005 follow 7669, 231006 and 231007 follow 14427 parts 1 to 7, and 231010 follows BS 7973. 5-10: the unit price of 231010 applies to every shape, diameter, rebar-mesh type and place of use and includes transport, placement and installation.
- 1-1-11 and 2-1-11: the profiles follow standard 12291 and the doors and windows 8510; the rubber sealing strip is E.P.D.M. 3-1-11: a steel frame is paid separately from the related rows of Chapter 16. 4-1-11 and 5-1-11: 231110 covers all door and window types and all profile types, including the galvanised reinforcing sheet, the rubber strip and the polyurethane injection foam. 8-1-11: 231110 is simple or printed; a coloured coating layer on one facade side adds 15 percent and on two facade sides 25 percent to the row price. 9-1-11: the hardware installation labour is included; the hardware procurement follows the general provisions of this pricebook.
- 1-13 and 2-13: the foams are approved by the consultant before procurement; 231310 is for under parquet and laminate. 3-13: the aluminium covering of 231315 is paid, with consultant approval, from the rows of Chapter 14.
- 1-14: the cold-rolled galvanised steel profiles used to install the facade pieces of 231401 to 231403 are paid from row 160701.
- 1-16 to 3-16: the wood-plastic composite 231610 follows standard 19905 and is for exterior surfaces; its substrate work is paid from the related rows.
- 1-17 to 4-17: the covers 231701 to 231708 follow standard 14976; the copolymer covers 231701 and 231702 and the composite covers 231707 and 231708 are inspection covers not under vehicle traffic; 231703 is the valve cover that rises automatically on overflow; 231706 is the surface-runoff grating. 5-17, 6-17 and 7-17: the rows are priced for class A15 and B125; class C250 or D400 adds 40 percent for 231701 and 231702, 60 percent for 231706 and 10 percent for 231707 and 231708. 8-17: the cover-setting masonry with sand-cement mortar is included; when the mortar grade is not specified it is one to five.
- 1-18 and 3-18: 231810 includes the consumables except the wallpaper and 231820 except the carpet; the wallpaper and carpet procurement follows the star-item instruction (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY). 2-18: the wallpaper adhesive follows standard 3773.
- 1-19 and 2-19: the GRP tanks follow standards 13709-1 and 13709-2; the panels are press-moulded per ISO 8605 with a surface roughness below 10 microns; the metal equipment is stainless or hot-galvanised steel with Dacromet-coated high-strength bolts; approvals and tests are required. 3-19: 231902 includes the installation, joint sealing, tank sealing and connection testing; the foundation is paid from the related rows; the hot-galvanised coating of the consumed steel, withstanding 300 hours of salt-spray per ISO 1461 or ASTM B117, is paid from rows 160230 and 160231 with coefficient one and one tenth; the bolted connections must withstand 400 hours of that test and are paid from row 091002 with coefficient one and two tenths; the stainless steel pieces are paid from row 160905.
- 1-20 to 3-20: the grating 232001 follows ASTM F3059; its substrate work is paid from the related rows; the metal clamps are included in the row price.

Thresholds and boundaries: the tolerance of one tenth of a millimetre (clause 3); P.V.C flooring one and a half to 2 mm with add-ons per millimetre beyond 2 up to 6 mm; sports P.V.C 4 to 5, more than 5 up to 6 and more than 6 up to 7 mm; rubber flooring nominal one and a half, two and a half, 3 and 4 mm; granular tiles up to 20 mm and per 10 mm beyond 20 up to 50 mm; base-bearing granular up to 40 mm and per 10 mm beyond 40 up to 65 mm; cast-in-place granular 10 mm and per 10 mm beyond 10 up to 30 mm; layered sports 12 to 16 mm; epoxy and polyurethane up to 2 mm and per millimetre beyond 2 up to 5 mm; skirting heights 7 to 9 and more than 9 up to 12 cm with thickness 4 to 8 mm; downpipe diameter up to 125 mm; sheet thicknesses 2, nominal 3, nominal 2 and nominal nine tenths of a millimetre; polypropylene sheets 300 grams per square metre with add-ons per 100 grams; nylon films about 150 and about 100 grams per square metre with the add-on of 230703 up to 200 grams; skylight bases up to one and six tenths of a square metre and beyond; bubbles up to five tenths, beyond five tenths up to one and three tenths and beyond one and three tenths up to 3 square metres; panels 6 to 10 mm and the U.P.V.C panel 20 to 22 cm wide and 2 mm thick; waterstop widths 19, 24 and 32 cm (hole type) and 24 and 32 cm (plain), bentonite 3 cm, hydrophilic 2 cm; drainage pipes of outside diameter 100 to 125 and 160 to 200 mm; EVA foam one and a half mm thick and 95 kilograms per cubic metre; polyethylene foam one mm thick and 25 kilograms per cubic metre; facade-piece coating 3 to 5 mm with total up to 55 mm and trim width up to 200 mm with the add-on per 100 mm beyond; cornice thickness 8 to 15 mm and widths up to 175 and beyond 175 up to 350 mm; cover diameters about 60 and about 80 cm, branch about 23 cm, water-inlet areas of forty-five to fifty-five hundredths of a square metre, composite cover areas of twenty-eight to thirty-six and beyond thirty-six up to sixty-four hundredths of a square metre, bucket diameter 50 to 70 cm; grating mesh 38 by 38 mm and thickness 25 mm with add-ons per 5 mm beyond 25 up to 40 mm; classes A15 and B125 versus C250 and D400; 300 and 400 hours of salt spray; roughness below 10 microns; turf height 45 to 55 mm and fibre weight at least 1200 grams per square metre; the colours named in 2-2. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: behaviour at the exact edges of the bands; the pro-rata treatment of fractional steps for 230351, 230361 and 231403 (the other step add-ons state it explicitly); the interpolation basis of 5-5; behaviour beyond the stated maxima, including 230531, which prints no upper bound; drainage pipe diameters between 125 and 160 mm; the combination order of the coefficients and percentages of 2-2, 3-3, 5-17, 6-17, 7-17, 8-1-11 and 3-19 with one another and with the add-on rows.

Price cells: 112 rows carry printed positive prices. Five rows have no printed price and each is INCOMPLETE, never priced from neighbouring rows: 231401, 231402, 231403, 231501 and 231502 (all the rows of groups 14 and 15). No cell reads one, and no negative price is printed. The add-on 230115 prints 62,100 rials, notably below its sibling add-ons; recorded as printed.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: clause 5-2 pays the initial geotextile layer from the rows of group twelve of this chapter, but the group table prints no group 12 (it runs 1 to 11 and then 13 to 20) and no 2312xx row is printed; the engine must not invent a group 12 or redirect the geotextile layer to another row, and the item remains INCOMPLETE. Clause 3-3 pays the fiberglass mesh from row 130406, but Chapter 13 prints 130403, 130404 and 130405 in that group and no 130406; the engine must not substitute 130405 or any other row, and the item remains INCOMPLETE. Groups 7 and 15 carry priced rows without a written requirements section, unlike the other seventeen groups. No duplicate code was found, and every other cited code is a printed row: 090701 and 091002 (Chapter 9) and 160701, 160230, 160231 and 160905 (Chapter 16); Chapters 14 and 16 are also cited generically (3-13, 3-1-11).

External references (material and technical only unless stated): national standards 10900, 10656, 18124, 16846, 21124-1, 21124-2, 19397, 12142-1, 1435, 16121, 21074, 16051, 19086, 13277-1, 13277-2, 17500, 12027, 7669, 14427 (parts 1 to 7), 12291, 8510, 19905, 14976, 3773, 13709-1 and 13709-2; BS EN 16153, BS 7973, ISO 8605, ISO 1461, ASTM B117 and ASTM F3059. The star-item instruction for the procurement of the wallpaper of 231810 and the carpet of 231820 is an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: those material prices cannot be formed without it. The general provisions of this pricebook govern the hardware procurement of 231110 and of the skylights (9-1-11, 12-5). Entries 32 to 37 of the book-wide bottom-of-page English glossary fall inside this chapter (Conductive, Antistatic, Unplasticized Poly Vinyl Chloride, Ethylene Propylene Diene Monomer, Ethylene-Vinyl Acetate, Grating); they are terminology aids and define no measurement or payment rule.

PDF layer note: the price tables set their Latin material designations in the same embedded font whose digit mapping is corrupted, and its letter mapping is corrupted as well; the text layer reads V.P.U, C.V.P.U, MDPE, MVnF, SBS, ScimPi and tSV there, while the font's own glyph assignments — cross-validated against the group tables and requirements, which are set in a reliable font, and against the terms themselves — read P.V.C, U.P.V.C, EPDM, SBR, Spacer and GRP, and the parentheticals of 230250, 230251 and 232001 read Monofilament, Fibrillate and Moulded Grating. Digits were read with the mapping validated in Phase 3.5-AX.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 23 rows and groups named; they do not leak to Chapter 22 or any other chapter. The Chapter 9, 13, 14 and 16 rows cited above are payment sources only. No rule of Chapters 16 to 22 is imported. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AE. Chapter 24 glass and its installation (Phase 3.5-AZ, printed pp. 205–210, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph from the chapter opening: «فصل بیست وچهارم. شیشه و نصب آن» (with a full stop after «وچهارم», on the opening page and in the running headers). Six pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی) clauses 1 to 10 (p205; clause 10 introduces the group table, groups 01 to 08); group requirements for all eight groups, with sub-clauses 1-1, 1-2 and 2-2, 1-3 and 2-3, 1-4, 1-5 to 4-5, 1-6 to 3-6, 1-7 to 6-7, and 1-8 and 2-8 with a printed worked example (مثال) (pp. 206–207); 43 price rows (pp. 208–210). Printed p211 opens «فصل بیست و پنجم. رنگ آمیزی», so Chapter 24 ends on p210. Every group has both written requirements and priced rows, and no group number is missing or duplicated. Codes, units and price cells were read glyph by glyph with the mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX and 3.5-AY; 40 rows are m² and 3 rows are metres (240605, 240725 and 240726). Rows 240102 to 240402 are printed on p208, rows 240410 to 240716 on p209 and rows 240720 to 240807 on p210:

| group                                                                                                 | rows and printed unit prices (rials)                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1 plain glass (شیشه ساده)                                                                             | 240102: 3,069,000; 240103: 3,707,000; 240104: 4,280,000; 240105: 4,861,000; 240106: 6,414,000; 240108: 7,002,000                                         |
| 2 figured glass (شیشه مشجر)                                                                           | 240201: 2,414,000; 240202: 3,056,000; 240204: 3,768,000; 240205: 4,212,000; 240211: 17,460,000                                                           |
| 3 heat-soaked tempered safety glass (شیشه ایمنی آبدیده حرارتی)                                        | 240301: 5,668,000; 240302: 6,688,000; 240303: 6,191,000; 240304: 7,913,000; 240305: 8,892,000; 240307: 10,755,000; 240308: 12,381,000; 240315: 2,261,000 |
| 4 coloured reflective glass (شیشه بازتابنده رنگی)                                                     | 240401: 4,932,000; 240402: 5,787,000; 240410: 1,069,000                                                                                                  |
| 5 glass tile and hollow glass block (کاشی شیشه‌ای و آجر شیشه‌ای توخالی)                               | 240501: 38,270,000; 240502: 25,954,000; 240505: 12,729,000; 240506: 32,595,000; 240507: 56,000,000; 240510: 1,832,000                                    |
| 6 frosting and edge-working of glass (مات کردن و لبه‌سازی شیشه)                                       | 240601: 2,997,000; 240602: 1,323,000; 240605: 568,500                                                                                                    |
| 7 coated, double- or triple-glazed glass and the like (شیشه‌های پوشش‌دار، دو جداره یا سه جداره و ...) | 240709: 874,000; 240710: 264,500; 240711: 120,000; 240715: 1,650,000; 240716: 529,000; 240720: 7,412,000; 240725: 1,117,000; 240726: 2,001,000           |
| 8 lamination (لایه‌کاری)                                                                              | 240804: 6,213,000; 240805: 7,313,000; 240806: 8,113,000; 240807: no printed price                                                                        |

Row descriptions: group 1 is supply and installation of plain glass of 4, 5, 6, 8, 10 and 12 millimetres; group 2 of figured glass of 4, 6, 8 and 10 millimetres, with 240211 the 6 millimetre wired figured glass; group 3 of heat-soaked tempered safety glass of 4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 12 and 15 millimetres; 240401 and 240402 are 4 and 6 millimetre reflective glass in the golden colour; 240501 and 240502 are glass tile of 15 by 15 and 20 by 20 centimetres on horizontal surfaces and 240505 of 20 by 20, 25 by 25 or 30 by 30 centimetres on vertical surfaces; 240506 and 240507 are hollow glass blocks of 20 by 20 and 20 by 10 centimetres, both of 8 centimetres thickness, on vertical surfaces; 240601 is sand-blasting (ماسه‌پاشی) and 240602 acid etching (اسیدشویی); 240605 is edge-working with final surface polishing per metre of glass edge; 240709, 240710 and 240711 are the silicone adhesive, plastic strip and بطانه for installing glass, each per square metre of installed glass; 240804, 240805 and 240806 laminate two flat glass panes with a polyvinyl butyral polymer layer of thirty-eight hundredths, seventy-six hundredths and one and fifty-two hundredths of a millimetre; 240807 laminates two flat glass panes with a fire-resistant layer.

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 240315 (installing fittings on heat-soaked tempered safety glass that is not installed inside a frame, fixed or operable), 240410 (reflective glass in a colour other than golden, added to 240401 and 240402), 240510 (coloured glass, added to 240501 to 240507), 240715 (coating with solar-heat transmission or reflection control, added to the plain or tempered supply-and-installation rows), 240716 (light-reflection coating, added to the plain-glass supply-and-installation rows), 240720 (enamelled glass, added to the plain or tempered supply-and-installation rows), 240725 (double glazing, per metre of the perimeter of the double-glazed unit) and 240726 (triple glazing, per metre of the perimeter of the triple-glazed unit). No deduction (کسر بها) row and no negative price is printed in this chapter.

Clause 14 relation: clause 1 states the chapter-wide basis, supplementing Clause 14: the measurement basis of the rows of this chapter is the glass surface that is installed. 4-5 (group 5): the measurement basis of rows 240501 to 240507 is the surface of the glass tile or hollow glass block itself. 2-6: rows 240601 and 240602 are measured on the entire glass surface of which all or a part is frosted. 2-7: rows 240715 and 240716 are counted for one glass surface and only once for double- or triple-glazed glass. 2-8: rows 240804 to 240806 are measured on the surface of the layer used for the lamination, and when the number of laminations exceeds one because of multiple panes, the row is counted per lamination. 5-7 with the row wording: the double- and triple-glazing add-ons 240725 and 240726 are per metre of the perimeter of the glazed unit, and the worked example computes 4 metres of 240725 for a one-by-one-metre pane. Rows 240709, 240710 and 240711 are per square metre of installed glass surface and 240605 is per metre of glass edge.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 2: the complete glass specifications — type, thickness, colour, double or triple glazing, lamination, coating and generally every cost-relevant information — must be stated in the technical specifications. Clause 3: all rows include measuring, cutting to size (قواره‌کردن), drilling for installing the fittings (یراق‌آلات), opening and re-closing the beads (زهوارها) and the other operations needed for complete execution. Clause 4: the silicone adhesive, plastic strip or بطانه for installing glass is paid, as applicable, from rows 240709 to 240711. Clause 7: glass installed inside a frame or without a frame, and the glass of glass railings, curtain walls (دیوارهای پرده‌ای) and the like, is paid, as applicable, from the rows of this same chapter. Clause 8: plain, heat-soaked tempered safety and reflective glass are of the float and flat type with straight cutting, and the prices of this chapter do not include bent glass or curved cutting (شیشه‌های خم و برش منحنی).
- Clause 5: the prices of this chapter, excepting the glass tile and hollow glass block rows, cover glass pieces each having an area of five hundredths of a square metre or more and a smallest surface dimension of at least 100 millimetres. Clause 6: when the plain or figured glass used in this chapter is coloured, 50 percent, and when the heat-soaked tempered safety glass is coloured, 30 percent, is added, as applicable, to the price of the related rows. Clause 9: the rows for radiation-shielding glass such as leaded glass (شیشه‌های سربی) must be prepared per the star-item instruction (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY).
- 1-1: the plain glass of rows 240102 to 240108 meets at least Iranian National Standard 10673-2 («شیشه ساختمانی- محصولات اولیه شیشه سیلیکاتی- سودا- لایم قسمت 2: شیشه فلوت»). 1-2 and 2-2: the figured glass of rows 240201 to 240205 meets 10673-5 and the wired figured glass of row 240211 meets 10673-6. 1-3: the heat-soaked tempered safety glass of rows 240301 to 240308 meets standard 2385. 2-3: for row 240315 the fittings cost follows the general provisions of this pricebook and the glass edge-working is calculated separately from row 240605.
- 1-4: rows 240401 and 240402 are reflective glass in which the light-reflecting property is created during production by adding carbon oxide, iron oxide or other secondary materials to the molten glass; when the property is created after production of the glass (جام شیشه) as a coating on the glass surface, the extra price of row 240716 is applied in addition to the related plain-glass supply-and-installation row.
- 1-5 and 2-5: the glass tile of rows 240501 to 240505 meets standard 14496 and the hollow glass block of rows 240506 and 240507 meets standard 3241. 3-5: the tile thickness of rows 240501 and 240502 is two and a half to 3 centimetres and of row 240505 is 1 to one and a half centimetres. 4-5: the mortar, grout, spacers, joint pointing and adhesive are included in rows 240501 to 240507; the steel mesh (شبکه فولادی) is paid separately from the related rows; the measurement basis of these rows is the surface of the tile or block itself.
- 1-6: frosting the glass surface, plain or banded (نواری), is paid, as applicable, from row 240601 or 240602. 3-6: row 240605 is for glass edge-working, whether angular bevel, half-round or full-round (پخ زاویه‌دار، نیم‌گرد یا تمام‌گرد) and پخ فارسیبر.
- 1-7: the coated glass of rows 240715 and 240716 complies with standard 16372. 2-7: for double- or triple-glazed glass these rows are counted for one glass surface and only once. 3-7: the enamel of row 240720 is ceramic enamel fired industrially onto one side of the glass. 4-7: row 240720 is not subject to the colour add-on of clause 6 of the General Requirements; its enamel is single-colour, and patterned enamel must be prepared per the star-item instruction (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY). 5-7: rows 240725 and 240726 meet at least standard 8521-1, are counted only when the glass is industrially double- or triple-glazed, and the price of each square metre of glass consumed is paid, by type and thickness, from the rows of this same chapter. 6-7: the cost of the air or the other gases between the panes is included in rows 240725 and 240726.
- 1-8 and 2-8: rows 240804 to 240806 meet at least standard 16373, are counted only when the glass is industrially laminated, and the price of each square metre of glass consumed is paid, by type and thickness, from the rows of this same chapter; the measurement basis is the surface of the lamination layer, counted per lamination when there is more than one.
- Worked example of 2-8: a double-glazed unit of one-by-one metres, with an inner pane of 6 millimetre heat-soaked tempered safety glass and an outer pane of two 6 millimetre plain float panes laminated with a polyvinyl butyral layer of one and fifty-two hundredths of a millimetre, the outer surface carrying a coating with solar-heat transmission control, is paid as one square metre from 240303, two square metres from 240104, 4 metres from 240725, one square metre from 240806 and one square metre from 240715.

Thresholds and boundaries: the clause 5 piece limits (area of five hundredths of a square metre or more and smallest surface dimension of at least 100 millimetres, the glass tile and hollow glass block rows excepted); the clause 6 colour add-ons of 50 and 30 percent; the tile thickness bands of 3-5; the tabulated glass thicknesses listed above; the polyvinyl butyral interlayer thicknesses of 240804, 240805 and 240806. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: the treatment of glass pieces below the clause 5 limits (no deduction, addition or separate payment is stated for them); whether the edges of the tile thickness bands are included; the combination order of the clause 6 percentages with the flat add-on rows and with each other; the perimeter basis of glazed units that are not rectangular. Clause 6 names only plain, figured and heat-soaked tempered safety glass: the reflective rows carry their own add-on 240410, the tile and block rows their own 240510 and 240720 is excluded by 4-7; the engine must not extend clause 6 to any other glass type.

Price cells: 42 rows carry printed positive prices. Row 240807 (لایه‌کاری دو شیشه مسطح با لایه مقاوم در برابر آتش — laminating two flat glass panes with a fire-resistant layer) prints the unit مترمربع and a blank price cell; it is INCOMPLETE — NO PRINTED PRICE, never priced from neighbouring rows. No cell reads one, and no negative price is printed.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: 240303, the 6 millimetre heat-soaked tempered safety glass, prints 6,191,000 rials, below the 6,688,000 rials of the 5 millimetre row 240302, breaking the otherwise thickness-monotone order of its group; both values are recorded as printed and neither is corrected. A Persian superscript footnote marker reading «75» is attached in clause 8 of the General Requirements with no footnote text printed anywhere in the chapter; it is content-free. The codes inside the groups are not contiguous (240107, 240203, 240306, 240309 to 240314, 240403 to 240409, 240503, 240504, 240508, 240509, 240603, 240604, 240701 to 240708, 240712 to 240714, 240717 to 240719, 240721 to 240724 and 240801 to 240803 are not printed); no missing code is inferred from the numerical sequences and no price is inferred for an unprinted code. Every code cited in the requirements and in the worked example is a printed row of this chapter; Chapter 24 cites no row of any other chapter.

External references (material and technical only unless stated): Iranian national standards 10673-2, 10673-5, 10673-6, 2385, 14496, 3241, 16372, 8521-1 and 16373. The star-item instruction for the radiation-shielding glass of clause 9 and for the patterned enamel of 240720 (4-7) is an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: those prices cannot be formed without it. The general provisions of this pricebook govern the fittings procurement of 240315 (2-3). Entry 38 of the book-wide bottom-of-page English glossary falls inside this chapter (Reflective, p205); it is a terminology aid and defines no measurement or payment rule.

PDF layer note: the digits of the requirements and of the price tables were read with the glyph mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX and 3.5-AY, because the embedded fonts lie about digits in the text layer (clause 3-6, for example, shows 536342 there where the glyph-validated code is 240605), and the split line of clause 1-4 citing row 240716 was verified glyph by glyph. The price tables of this chapter contain no Latin material designation, so no Latin letter decoding was needed; the only Latin text in the chapter is the glossary entry. The bottom glossary and the superscript footnote marker are content-free.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 24 rows and groups named; they do not leak to Chapter 23 or any other chapter, and no rule of Chapters 16 to 23 is imported. Chapter 24 cites no row of any other chapter; its only external payment references are the star-item instruction and the general provisions of this pricebook. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AF. Chapter 25 painting (Phase 3.5-BA, printed pp. 211–218, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph from the chapter opening: «فصل بیست و پنجم. رنگ آمیزی» (with a full stop after «پنجم»; «رنگ آمیزی» carries a plain space on the opening page and in the running headers). Eight pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی) clauses 1 to 3 (p211; clause 1 carries sub-clauses 1-1 to 1-4; clause 3 introduces the group table, which prints groups 01 and 03 to 08, seven groups with no group 02); group requirements for all seven groups, with sub-clauses 1-1 to 6-1, 1-3 to 10-3 including two printed worked examples (مثال), 1-4 to 4-4, 1-5 to 3-5, 1-6 and 2-6, 1-7 to 3-7, and 1-8 (pp. 211–214); 45 price rows (pp. 215–218). Printed p219 opens «فصل بیست وششم. زیراساس و اساس» (the opening page prints «زیراساس» as one word while the running headers print «زیر اساس و اساس»), so Chapter 25 ends on p218. Codes, units and price cells were read glyph by glyph with the mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-AZ; 42 rows are m² and 3 rows are metres (250601, 250602 and 250604). Rows 250101 to 250330 are printed on p215, rows 250331 to 250405 on p216, rows 250406 to 250706 on p217 and rows 250707 to 250802 on p218:

| group                                                                         | rows and printed unit prices (rials)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1 preparation and descaling (آمادهسازی و زنگزدایی)                            | 250101: 265,500; 250103: 569,000; 250104: 609,500                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 3 painting on steel surfaces (رنگآمیزی روی سطوح فولادی)                       | 250304: 1,685,000; 250305: 1,791,000; 250320: 1,216,000; 250321: 1,134,000; 250322: 1,181,000; 250323: 1,183,000; 250324: 1,173,000; 250325: 1,152,000; 250326: 1,152,000; 250330: 1,113,000; 250331: 1,074,000; 250332: 1,242,000; 250333: 1,156,000; 250334: 1,116,000; 250335: 1,191,000; 250336: 1,057,000; 250340: 1,133,000; 250345: 1,565,000; 250350: 1,625,000 |
| 4 painting on wooden surfaces (رنگآمیزی روی سطوح چوبی)                        | 250401: 1,871,000; 250403: 1,961,000; 250404: 2,077,000; 250405: 9,848,000; 250406: 6,517,000                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 5 painting on gypsum surfaces (رنگآمیزی روی سطوح گچی)                         | 250501: 1,193,000; 250502: 960,000; 250503: 420,500; 250506: 828,500; 250507: 936,000; 250508: 172,500                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 6 painting with traffic paints (رنگآمیزی با رنگهای ترافیکی)                   | 250601: 147,500; 250602: 143,000; 250603: 6,319,000; 250604: 14,800                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 7 painting on cement or concrete surfaces (رنگآمیزی روی سطوح سیمانی یا بتنی)  | 250701: 682,500; 250703: 518,500; 250705: 519,000; 250706: 336,500; 250707: 1,130,000; 250708: 1,093,000                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 8 painting with fire-resistant paints (رنگآمیزی با رنگهای مقاوم در برابر آتش) | 250801: no printed price; 250802: no printed price                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Row descriptions: group 1 is preparation or descaling of steel skeletons with sandpaper or wire brush (250101), by sand blasting (250103) and by shot blasting (250104). Group 3: 250304 and 250305 are the complete oil-based and complete gold-size (اکلیلی) paintings on metal works including the anti-rust preparation; 250320 to 250326, 250330 to 250336, 250340, 250345 and 250350 are single-coat airless applications at a dry thickness of 40 micron on the steel skeleton: alkyd (250320), iron-oxide alkyd in ochre (250321), zinc-rich alkyd (250322), zinc-phosphate alkyd (250323), zinc-chromate alkyd (250324), zinc-chromate alkyd in ochre (250325), lead-oxide alkyd in ochre (250326), two-component epoxy polyamide (250330) with its iron-oxide variant (250331), zinc-rich (250332), zinc-phosphate (250333) and zinc-chromate (250334) variants, two-component epoxy polyamine (250335), one-component epoxy ester (250336), two-component polyurethane (250340), two-component ethyl-silicate zinc (250345) and one-component vinyl (250350). Group 4: complete oil painting on doors and other wooden works (250401), alcohol-lacquer painting (لاک الکل, 250403), complete sealer and clear (250404), polyester painting (250405) and semi-polyester painting (250406). Group 5: oil (250501), plastic (250502) and semi-plastic (250503) painting on gypsum surfaces or gypsum boards of walls and ceilings, fireproof gold-size (اکلیل نسوز) including primer and finish (250506), acrylic including primer and finish (250507), and the special primer for gypsum boards before paint, wallpaper, ceramic, stone and the like (250508). Group 6: dashed-and-alternating (250601) and continuous (250602) line marking of 12 centimetre width with cold traffic paints, two-component cold paint on asphalt and concrete surfaces such as pedestrian crossings (250603), and the add-on for machinery other than vehicle machinery (250604). Group 7: oil painting on cement or concrete surfaces including primer and finish (250701), acrylic with one primer, one undercoat and one finish coat (250703), plastic (250705) and semi-plastic (250706) painting, resin-based combined cladding (نماسازی رزینی ترکیبی) of the long-oil alkyd type (250707) and of the emulsion copolymer type (250708). Group 8: water-based (250801) and solvent-based (250802) fire-resistant spray paints (رنگ پفکننده).

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 250604, applied to rows 250601 and 250602 when machinery and tools other than vehicle machinery are used. No deduction (کسر بها) row and no negative price is printed in this chapter.

Clause 14 relation: general clause 1 governs the measurement of painting surfaces through its sub-clauses and supplements Clause 14; no rule replaces it. 1-1: painted and descaled surfaces are measured. 1-2: prefabricated members such as railings, windows, metal or wooden trusses and meshes are counted one side, unless the developed painted surface of the members exceeds the one-side surface, in which case the developed painted surface is the basis. 1-3: the glazed area of doors is deducted from both sides, unless the glazed area exceeds half the door area, in which case the door is counted one side like a window. 1-4: the surface of rebar is measured per its apparent surface on the basis of the nominal diameter. 1-6 (group 6): dashed and alternating line marking is measured on the painted length.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 2: the complete painting specifications — paint type, execution method, colour shade (فام) and procurement source, and generally every cost-relevant information — must be stated in the technical specifications.
- General 1-2: to compensate the execution difficulty of painting the prefabricated members of that sub-clause, 50 percent is added to the price of the related rows. Whether this addition applies to every prefabricated member of the sub-clause or only to the case where the developed surface governs is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK; the engine must not extend it beyond those members.
- 1-1: preparation in rows 250101 to 250104 means cleaning the steel surfaces of rolling oxide, salts, dust, oil and grease, and must meet at least نشریه 306 «آمادهسازی و تمیزکاری سطوح فلزی جهت اجرای پوشش» (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY). 2-1: descaling is paid, as applicable, from the descaling rows only when the consultant engineer diagnoses it and the employer approves. 3-1: row 250101 is set at cleaning grade St 2 and rows 250103 and 250104 at Sa two and a half; when the technical specifications require sand or shot blasting at grade Sa 2, the related rows apply with coefficient nine tenths. 4-1: rows 250101 to 250104 serve the members of the steel skeleton (the subject of the rows of Chapter 9); the preparation and descaling of rebar and other steel members is paid, as applicable, from the related rows with coefficient one and eight tenths. 5-1: rows 250101 and 250103 serve descaling of the steel skeleton before erection; descaling on erected skeleton surfaces is paid from the related rows with coefficient one and three tenths. 6-1: the cost of post-erection descaling touch-up is included in the row prices.
- 1-3 (group 3): the anti-rust preparation and execution costs are included in rows 250304 and 250305. 2-3: the stages of oil or gold-size painting on steel works are sanding or brushing, anti-rust, puttying, sanding, oil or gold-size primer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups and the finish coat. 3-3: rows 250320 to 250350 must meet at least Iranian National Standard 6594 «حفاظت سازههای فولادی در برابر خوردگی با استفاده از سیستم رنگهای محافظ» (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY). 4-3: the paint type, number of coats, thickness of each coat and application method of rows 250320 to 250350 must be stated in the technical specifications. 5-3: when the thickness of each coat per the technical specifications is less than or more than 40 micron relative to the thickness stated in the rows of this group, and at most 60 micron, then for every 10 micron, 20 percent is added to or deducted from the unit price of the related rows; a deduction of 10 micron is computed pro rata. 6-3: rows 250320 to 250350 serve the steel skeleton (the subject of the rows of Chapter 9) and the light steel works (the subject of the rows of Chapter 16); painting rebar (the subject of the rows of Chapter 7) is paid from the related rows with coefficient one and eight tenths. 7-3: those rows serve painting before erection; painting erected surfaces per the consultant engineer's instruction is paid from the related rows with coefficient one and three tenths. 8-3: the cost of post-erection painting touch-up is included in the row prices. 9-3: execution by a method other than the airless method takes the related rows with coefficient sixty-five hundredths. 10-3: row 250321, the iron-oxide alkyd, is the ordinary anti-rust paint in the ochre colour.
- Worked example 1 of 5-3: a three-coat system with a first coat of zinc-rich epoxy polyamide at 60 micron, a second coat of epoxy polyamide at 40 micron and a third coat of polyurethane at 30 micron. The printed formula for the first coat computes the bracket of 60 minus 40 divided by 10, times two tenths, equals four tenths, so 40 percent is added to row 250332; the second coat is taken from row 250330; the printed formula for the third coat computes the bracket of 30 minus 40 divided by 10, times two tenths, equals minus two tenths, so 80 percent of the price of row 250340 is considered. Worked example 2 of 5-3: a two-coat system with a first coat of zinc-chromate alkyd at 100 micron and a second coat of alkyd at 40 micron. Up to 60 micron the same formula gives four tenths, so 40 percent is added to row 250324, and the remaining thickness from 60 to 100 micron is taken again from row 250324; in total, for the 100 micron thickness, 240 percent of the price of row 250324 is considered; the second coat is taken from row 250320.
- 1-4 to 4-4 (group 4): the printed stage lists of the wooden-surface paintings: oil — sanding, roghan-alif, puttying, sanding, primer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups, finish; alcohol lacquer — liseh work, sanding, puttying, sanding, lacquer-and-alcohol primer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups, lacquer finish and its polishing; sealer and clear — liseh work, sanding, puttying, sanding, sealer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups, clear; polyester — skinning, puttying, polyester paint, skinning with the needed grades, red polish, white polish and polishing milk, finishing.
- 1-5 to 3-5 (group 5): the printed stage lists of the gypsum-surface paintings: oil — sanding, roghan-alif, puttying, sanding, primer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups, finish; plastic — sanding, roghan-alif, puttying, sanding, plastic primer, putty touch-up, sanding of the touch-ups, finish; semi-plastic — sanding, putty touch-up, primer, finish.
- 2-6 (group 6): rows 250601 and 250602 must be executed with vehicle machinery; when the work conditions require and the consultant engineer approves the use of machinery and tools other than vehicle machinery, the add-on 250604 applies.
- 1-7 to 3-7 (group 7): the material compositions as printed — 250703 for cement and concrete surfaces comprises emulsion acrylic resin, titanium oxide, calcium carbonate and other minor materials; 250707 is the combined resin cladding of the long-oil alkyd type for exterior or interior use, its resin carrying 60 to 70 percent oil and 20 to 30 percent phthalic anhydride, with titanium oxide, calcium carbonate, a petroleum-based solvent and minor materials, factory-produced; 250708 is the emulsion-type resin cladding comprising emulsion resins such as polyvinyl acetate, titanium oxide, calcium carbonate and minor materials, factory-produced.
- 1-8 (group 8): when rows 250801 and 250802 are used, the required specifications must be stated in the technical specifications and the unit price of the related rows is determined per the star-item instruction (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY).

Thresholds and boundaries: the 1-2 one-side rule with the developed surface governing only when it exceeds the one-side surface (at equality the one-side measure stands); the 1-3 door rule with the one-side counting only when the glazed area exceeds half the door area (at exactly half the glazing is deducted from both sides); the cleaning grades St 2, Sa two and a half and Sa 2 of 3-1; the 5-3 thickness adjustment with its 40 micron basis, its maximum of 60 micron, its step of 10 micron at 20 percent and its pro-rata deduction of 10 micron (the worked examples reach down to 30 micron and up to 100 micron by repeating the row); the 12 centimetre line width of 250601 and 250602; the 60 to 70 percent oil and 20 to 30 percent phthalic-anhydride composition of 250707. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: the lower bound of the 5-3 adjustment below 40 micron (the clause states no minimum and the example reaches 30 micron); the treatment of coat thickness beyond 60 micron other than the printed hundred-micron example, and whether the row repetition of that example generalises; the combination order of the coefficients of 3-1, 4-1, 5-1, 6-3, 7-3 and 9-3 with one another, with the 50 percent addition of 1-2 and with the 20 percent adjustment of 5-3; the scope of the 50 percent addition (see above).

Price cells: 43 rows carry printed positive prices. Rows 250801 and 250802 print the unit مترمربع and blank price cells; each is INCOMPLETE — NO PRINTED PRICE, never priced from neighbouring rows, and consistent with 1-8 their prices follow the star-item instruction. No cell reads one, and no negative price is printed.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the group table prints groups 01 and 03 to 08 with no group 02, no requirements section for a group 2 is printed and no 2502xx row is printed; the engine must not invent group 2 or any 2502xx row. Sub-clauses 1-1 and 4-1 cite the range «250101 تا 250104», but 250102 is not printed as a row; the engine must not invent 250102 or redirect the citation to another row. The ranges cited in 3-3, 4-3, 5-3, 6-3 and 7-3 (250320 تا 250350) span codes that are not printed as rows, such as 250327 to 250329, 250337 to 250339, 250341 to 250344 and 250346 to 250349; the printed rows inside the range are those listed above and no missing code or price is inferred. Persian superscript footnote markers appear in clause 3-1 (p212, four digits in split pairs) and in clause 9-3 (p213, reading «79»); no footnote text is printed anywhere in the chapter, so they are content-free. Row 250345 prints «اجرایرنگ» without the intervening space (a printed typo; code, unit and price are unaffected). Rows 250325 and 250326 both print 1,152,000 rials; recorded as printed. No duplicate code was found.

External references (material and technical only unless stated): نشریه 306 «آمادهسازی و تمیزکاری سطوح فلزی جهت اجرای پوشش» (1-1) and Iranian National Standard 6594 «حفاظت سازههای فولادی در برابر خوردگی با استفاده از سیستم رنگهای محافظ» (3-3) are EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. The star-item instruction for 250801 and 250802 (1-8) is an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: those prices cannot be formed without it. Chapters 7, 9 and 16 are cited as subject references only (rebar, steel skeleton, light steel works); no row of another chapter is cited and no rule of those chapters is imported. The St and Sa cleaning grades print in Latin without the source naming a defining standard; no external definition is imported for them. Entries 39 to 41 of the book-wide bottom-of-page English glossary fall inside this chapter (Sand blasting, Shot blasting, Air Less); they are terminology aids and define no measurement or payment rule.

PDF layer note: the digits of the requirements and of the price tables were read with the glyph mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-AZ, because the embedded fonts lie about digits in the text layer (clause 3-1 on p212, for example, shows codes 253131, 253133 and 253134 there where the glyph-validated codes are 250101, 250103 and 250104). The St and Sa notations and the glossary entries are set in Times New Roman, whose text layer is reliable; the price tables contain no Latin material designation, so no Latin letter decoding was needed. The superscript footnote markers and the bottom glossary are content-free.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 25 rows and groups named; they do not leak to Chapter 24 or any other chapter, and no rule of Chapters 16 to 24 is imported. The Chapters 7, 9 and 16 references are subject references only. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AG. Chapter 26 sub-base and base (Phase 3.5-BB, printed pp. 219–222, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph from the chapter opening: «فصل بیست وششم. زیراساس و اساس» («زیراساس» printed as one word on the opening page; glyph-level gap analysis shows the running headers also print it as one word, and the space sometimes shown by the text layer is an extraction artifact — the parenthetical in block AF records the text-layer form and is left unchanged). Four pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی) clauses 1 to 7 (p219; clause 7 introduces the group table, which prints groups 01, 03, 04, 06, 07 and 08 — six groups with no group 02 and no group 05); group requirements for groups 6, 7 and 8 only — the group 6 requirements are a single unnumbered paragraph, group 7 carries sub-clauses 1-7 and 2-7 plus footnote 83 printed with its text, and group 8 carries sub-clauses 1-8 and 2-8 (pp. 219–220); 16 price rows (pp. 221–222). Printed p223 opens «فصل بیست وهفتم. آسفالت», so Chapter 26 ends on p222. Codes, units and price cells were read glyph by glyph with the mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-BA; all 16 rows are cubic metres (مترمکعب). Rows 260101 to 260604 are printed on p221 and rows 260605 to 260803 on p222:

| group                                                                                                         | rows and printed unit prices (rials)                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1 procurement of sub-base material (تهیه مصالح زیر اساس)                                                      | 260101: 4,864,000; 260102: 5,024,000; 260103: 5,221,000            |
| 3 procurement of base material (تهیه مصالح اساس)                                                              | 260301: 5,571,000; 260302: 5,599,000; 260303: 5,674,000            |
| 4 percent-crush add-on (اضافه‌بها درصد شکستگی)                                                                | 260401: price cell reads one                                       |
| 6 spreading, watering, leveling and compacting sub-base and base (پخش، آبپاشی، تسطیح و کوبیدن زیراساس و اساس) | 260601: 564,000; 260603: 508,500; 260604: 769,000; 260605: 713,000 |
| 7 procurement of touneaux and wind-blown sand (تهیه توونان و ماسه بادی)                                       | 260701: 4,855,000; 260702: 3,797,000                               |
| 8 stabilisation and reinforcement of the subgrade and sub-base (تثبیت و تقویت زیرسازی و زیراساس)              | 260801: 1,543,000; 260802: 1,543,000; 260803: 1,699,000            |

Row descriptions: the group 1 rows are the procurement of sub-base material, loading, haulage and unloading at the place of use, when the material gradation is 0 to 50 (260101), 0 to 38 (260102) and 0 to 25 (260103) millimetres. The group 3 rows are the same for base material, when the gradation is 0 to 50 (260301), 0 to 38 (260302) and 0 to 25 (260303) millimetres and at least 50 percent of the material retained on sieve No. 4 is crushed on one face. 260401 is an add-on to rows 260301 to 260303 when the percent crush of the material on sieve No. 4 exceeds 50 percent, per each 5 percent of extra crush, one time. The group 6 rows are spreading, watering, leveling and compacting sub-base layers of thickness up to 15 centimetres (260601) and more than 15 up to 20 centimetres (260603), and base layers of thickness up to 10 centimetres (260604) and more than 10 up to 15 centimetres (260605), each with at least 100 percent compaction by the modified AASHTO method (آشتو اصلاحی). 260701 is the procurement of touneaux (توونان) and 260702 of wind-blown sand (ماسه بادی), each with loading, haulage and unloading at the place of use. The group 8 rows are the stabilisation and reinforcement of the subgrade, sub-base and the like by mixing soil and slaked lime to a thickness of up to 15 centimetres with a content of 50 kilograms of slaked lime per cubic metre of stabilised material, including the procurement and haulage of the slaked lime, screening, spreading and mixing, watering and compacting, at 90 percent compaction (260801), 95 percent (260802) and 100 percent (260803) by the modified AASHTO method.

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 260401. No deduction (کسر بها) row and no negative price is printed in this chapter.

Clause 14 relation: clause 1 supplements Clause 14 with the chapter-wide basis: the measurement of all rows of this chapter is on the executed work per the technical specifications, work orders or minutes (the source prints a stray conjunction, «یا و», before «صورتجلسهها»); no separate payment arises on account of volume change from settlement or compaction, and the volume of the layers after compaction is the operative basis for computing the quantities.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 2: the rows include procurement, loading and haulage up to 30 kilometres from the production place and unloading at the place of use; haulage beyond 30 kilometres is considered per the related rows of the transport chapter (Chapter 28 — payment reference only); when loading, haulage and unloading take place in multiple passes, no separate payment is made for the extra loading, haulage and unloading. Clause 3: the complete material specifications — material type, gradation and percent crush, procurement source and generally every cost-relevant information — must be stated in the technical specifications. Clause 4: the material of the sub-base and base layers is river material (رودخانهای); when mountain material (کوهی) is specified per the technical specifications, it is likewise estimated and considered per the rows of this chapter. Clause 5: the crush of the stone materials in the rows of this chapter means artificial crushing; natural crushing is not meant. Clause 6: the cost of procuring and hauling the consumed water is included in the row prices.
- Group 6 requirements (unnumbered paragraph): for compaction below 100 percent, in conformity with the Code for Design and Construction of Rural Roads (نشریه شماره 862), a price deduction equivalent to 1 percent per each percent of reduction applies to rows 260601, 260603, 260604 and 260605.
- 1-7: the costs of spreading, watering and compacting the touneaux of row 260701 are considered from the related rows of Chapter 3 (payment reference only). 2-7: the costs of spreading, leveling, flooding and compacting the wind-blown sand of row 260702 are considered from the related rows of Chapter 3 (payment reference only). Footnote 83, printed with its text on p219, defines توونان: a coarse river-run material containing relatively small amounts of fines (passing sieve No. 200), commonly used in fills without gradation correction; its gradation must be such that in the Unified (یونیفاید) classification it classifies with the G suffix.
- 1-8: the soil needed for the stabilisation and reinforcement of the subgrade, sub-base and the like is procured from the borrow pit (inside or outside the site) and/or from the existing bed soil after ploughing, digging, or a mixture of them; the digging, ploughing, procurement and haulage of suitable soil is considered from the related rows of Chapter 3 (payment reference only). 2-8: when the quantity of lime per cubic metre of stabilised material is more than the figures stated in the row descriptions of this group, the extra lime is computed and considered per row 110904 (verified as a printed row of Chapter 11, block R — payment reference only; no rule of Chapter 11 is imported).

Thresholds and boundaries: the gradation bands 0 to 50, 0 to 38 and 0 to 25 millimetres; at least 50 percent of the sieve-No. 4 retained material crushed on one face; the 260401 add-on per each 5 percent of crush beyond 50 percent, one time each; the layer thickness bands up to 15, more than 15 up to 20, up to 10 and more than 10 up to 15 centimetres, with the edges as printed (تا includes the named upper value and بیش از excludes the named lower value); at least 100 percent modified-AASHTO compaction for the group 6 rows; the 90, 95 and 100 percent compaction levels of the group 8 rows; the 50 kilograms of slaked lime per cubic metre of stabilised material; the 30-kilometre haulage inclusion; the group 6 deduction of 1 percent per each percent of compaction reduction. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: the treatment of a fractional part of the 5-percent step of 260401; the treatment of a fractional percent in the group 6 deduction; the combination order of the 260401 add-on with the group 6 deduction and with the beyond-30-kilometre haulage of Chapter 28; the test procedure or acceptance method behind the compaction percentages (the source names only the modified AASHTO method and نشریه 862 and prints no test procedure).

External references (payment references and material/technical only unless stated): نشریه 862 «آیین نامه طراحی و اجرای معابر درون روستایی» (group 6 deduction) is an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY for its compaction provisions. Chapter 3 is cited generically for the spreading and compaction of 260701 and 260702 and for the soil works of 1-8; no specific row is named. Chapter 28 is cited generically for haulage beyond 30 kilometres. Row 110904 of Chapter 11 is cited by number and verified printed. The modified AASHTO method and the Unified G classification are named without a standard number; no external values or procedures are imported for them.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the group table prints groups 01, 03, 04, 06, 07 and 08 with no group 02 and no group 05; no requirements section and no row is printed for either; the engine must not invent them. Groups 1, 3 and 4 carry priced rows without a written requirements section, unlike groups 6, 7 and 8. The price cell of 260401 reads one, a suspicious placeholder; it is INCOMPLETE — SUSPICIOUS PRICE CELL, never priced from neighbouring rows. Rows 260801 and 260802 print the identical price 1,543,000 rials although their compaction percentages differ (90 and 95 percent); recorded as printed. The codes skip 260104 onwards, 260304 onwards, 260602, 260606 onwards, 260703 onwards and 260804 onwards; no missing code or price is inferred from the sequences. Clause 1 prints the stray conjunction «یا و» before «صورتجلسهها». The group 6 requirements carry no clause numbers.

PDF layer note: the digits of the requirements and of the price tables were read with the glyph mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-BA, and every code and price was verified at glyph level (the price of 260401 is a single digit one, HM FLotoos gid 464). The only Latin letter in the chapter is the G of the Unified classification inside footnote 83, set in Times New Roman, whose text layer is reliable. Unlike Chapters 22 to 25, the footnote marker 83 is printed with its footnote text.

Isolation: these rules apply only to the Chapter 26 rows and groups named; they do not leak to Chapter 25 or any other chapter, and no rule of Chapters 16 to 25 is imported. The Chapter 3 and Chapter 28 references and row 110904 are payment references only. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AH. Chapter 27 asphalt (Phase 3.5-BC, printed pp. 223–227, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph from the chapter opening: «فصل بیست وهفتم. آسفالت» (identical wording on the opening page and in the running headers). Five pages: General Requirements (الزامات عمومی) clauses 1 to 9 (p223; clause 9 introduces the group table, which prints groups 01 to 06 contiguously); group requirements for groups 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 only, with sub-clauses 1-1, 1-2 and 2-2, 1-4 and 2-4, 1-5, and 1-6 (p224); 29 price rows (pp. 225–227). Printed p228 opens «فصل بیست و هشتم. حمل و نقل», so Chapter 27 ends on p227. Codes, units and price cells were read glyph by glyph with the mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-BB; 8 rows are kilograms, 3 are square metres (270210, 270501 and 270502), 16 are cubic metres and 2 are cubic decimetres (270503 and 270510). Rows 270101 to 270320 are printed on p225, rows 270402 to 270602 on p226 and rows 270603 and 270604 on p227:

| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| 2 surface-coat add-on and cleaning beneath the coat (اضافه‌بهای اندود سطحی و تمیزکاری زیر اندود)                       | 270202: 22,900; 270210: 35,400                                                                                        |
| 3 asphaltic concrete (بتن آسفالتی)                                                                                     | 270301: 48,586,000; 270302: 48,920,000; 270303: 50,851,000; 270304: 52,806,000; 270310: 709,000; 270320: -1,037,000   |
| 4 add-ons and deductions of the asphalt rows (اضافه‌بها و کسر‌بها مربوط به ردیفهای آسفالت)                             | 270402: 2,131,000; 270403: -2,131,000; 270404: 18,392,000; 270410: 9,985,000; 270411: 6,886,000; 270412: 3,170,000    |
| 5 roof asphalt, sand-asphalt joint filling and bituminous mastic (آسفالت بام، پرکردن درز با ماسه آسفالت و ماستیک قیری) | 270501: 1,443,000; 270502: 530,500; 270503: 161,500; 270510: 1,037,000                                                |
| 6 cold asphalt (آسفالت سرد)                                                                                            | 270601: 33,486,000; 270602: 34,139,000; 270603: 35,171,000; 270604: 5,113,000                                         |

Row descriptions: 270101 is the prime coat with cutback bitumen (قیر محلول); 270102 to 270107 are prime coats with bitumen emulsion — cationic CSS of at least 57 percent residual bitumen in the distillation test (270102), cationic CMS of at least 65 percent (270103), cationic CRS of at least 60 percent (270104), anionic SS of at least 57 percent (270105), anionic MS of at least 55 percent (270106) and anionic RS of at least 55 percent (270107); the designations were read at glyph level (see the PDF layer note). 270202 is an add-on to rows 270101 to 270107 when a surface coat is executed; 270210 is the cleaning and preparation of the surface beneath the coat. The group 3 rows are asphaltic concrete with crushed stone for the binder course (قشر آستر) when the material gradation is 0 to thirty-seven and a half (270301), 0 to 25 (270302), 0 to 19 (270303) and 0 to twelve and a half (270304) millimetres; 270310 is an add-on to rows 270303 and 270304 when the asphaltic concrete is executed in the wearing course (قشر رویه); 270320 is a deduction from rows 270301 and 270302 when the asphaltic concrete is executed in the bituminous base course (قشر اساس قیر). 270402 adds, and 270403 deducts, per each 10 kilograms of bitumen consumed in excess or shortfall per cubic metre of asphalt per clause 6 of the chapter introduction (a fractional 10 kilograms is computed pro rata); 270404 is an add-on when the asphalt is executed on sidewalks, passages and the like at a width of up to 2 metres; 270410 to 270412 are patching add-ons when the patch area is 20 square metres and less (270410), more than 20 up to 50 (270411) and more than 50 up to 100 (270412). 270501 is roof asphalt of one and a half to 2 centimetres thickness; 270502 is an add-on to 270501 per each centimetre of thickness increase (a fractional centimetre is computed pro rata); 270503 is the procurement of materials and filling of joints with sand asphalt; 270510 is bituminous sealing mastic for use in joints with complete final finishing of the surface. The group 6 rows are cold in-place mixed asphalt with crushed stone of continuous gradation 0 to 25 (270601), 0 to 19 (270602) and 0 to twelve and a half (270603) millimetres; 270604 is an add-on to rows 270601 to 270603 when the cold asphalt is factory-made.

Add-on rows (اضافه‌بها): 270202, 270310, 270402, 270404, 270410, 270411, 270412, 270502 and 270604. Deduction rows (کسر بها): 270320 and 270403; each prints a genuinely negative price (minus one million thirty-seven thousand rials for 270320 and minus two million one hundred thirty-one thousand rials for 270403), classified per their printed wording as deductions from the rows they name, never as costs, and never inputs to a price.

Clause 14 relation: clause 4 supplements Clause 14 with the chapter-wide basis: the measurement of all rows of this chapter is on the executed work per the technical specifications; no separate payment arises from the volume change due to compaction; the volume of the layers after compaction is the operative basis for computing the quantities; and when the thickness excess or deficit is within the permitted range, it is added to or deducted from the work price in the same proportion. The permitted range itself is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK — the source prints only «در حد مجاز» and no limit; the engine must not invent a tolerance or a limit.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named:

- Clause 1: the stone material consumed in the rows of this chapter is river material; when mountain material is specified per the technical specifications, it is likewise estimated and considered per the rows of this chapter. Clause 2: when the use of additives is necessary at the consultant engineer's proposal and the employer's approval, the related rows must be prepared per the star-item instruction (EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY), with the complete specifications of the needed additives stated in the technical specifications. Clause 3: the asphalt must be prepared per the specifications stated in the technical specifications; before preparation, the type and gradation of the stone materials, the type and quantity of the bitumen and, if needed, the additives, and finally the mix design (طرح اختلاط) must reach the consultant engineer's approval; the approved mix design is communicated to the contractor for execution. Clause 5: the crush of the stone materials means artificial crushing, natural crushing is not meant, and the number of faces and the crush quantity follow the technical specifications, with no extra payment on this account. Clause 6: the bitumen consumed per cubic metre included in the row prices is 90 kilograms for 270301, 110 kilograms for 270302 and 270303, 120 kilograms for 270304 and 90 kilograms for cold in-place mixed asphalt; when the bitumen quantity changes per the approved mix design, each 10 kilograms of excess or shortfall per cubic metre relative to those figures is computed, respectively, from rows 270402 and 270403. Clause 7: the asphaltic-concrete rows include the procurement, loading and haulage of the materials up to 30 kilometres from the production place and the unloading at the place of use; the haulage beyond 30 kilometres is considered from row 280301 (Chapter 28 — payment reference only). Clause 8: the complete specifications of the cutback and emulsified bitumens, their procurement source and generally every cost-relevant information must be stated in the technical specifications.
- 1-1: the group 1 rows include the bitumen procurement price, loading, haulage and unloading at the place of use. 1-2: row 270210 includes sweeping and cleaning the surface beneath the coat with the mechanical broom, compressor and, if needed, the water-sprinkling machine. 2-2: applying the coat-execution rows (prime or surface) is conditional on the execution of row 270210.
- 1-4: the add-on rows 270404 to 270412 do not apply to roof asphalt. 2-4: when the prices of rows 270410 to 270412 are applied, the price of row 270404 is not considered.
- 1-5: the price of row 270510 includes execution on all surfaces and by any method, for filling and sealing all types of joints.
- 1-6: the cold-asphalt rows 270601 to 270603 include the procurement, loading and haulage of the materials from the production place and the unloading at the place of use; only the stone-material haulage beyond 30 kilometres is considered per the related rows of the transport chapter; when factory cold asphalt is used, the haulage beyond 30 kilometres is considered, as applicable, from rows 280301 and 280302 (Chapter 28 — payment reference only), and no other separate haulage payment is made for the stone materials.

Thresholds and boundaries: the gradation bands 0 to thirty-seven and a half, 0 to 25, 0 to 19 and 0 to twelve and a half millimetres; the residual-bitumen minimums of the six emulsions in the distillation test; the 10-kilogram bitumen step with its pro-rata fractional treatment; the 30-kilometre haulage inclusion; the patch-area bands of 20 square metres and less, more than 20 up to 50 and more than 50 up to 100; the sidewalk width of up to 2 metres; the roof-asphalt thickness of one and a half to 2 centimetres with its per-centimetre add-on and pro-rata fractional treatment; the bitumen contents of clause 6. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: the permitted range of the clause 4 thickness excess or deficit; the combination order of the add-ons and deductions with one another (for example 270310 or 270320 with 270402 or 270403, and either with 270404 or the patching add-ons); whether the patch-area bands and the width condition of 270404 can apply simultaneously; the treatment of patch areas above 100 square metres; the application rate of the prime and surface coats.

External references (payment references and acceptance requirements only unless stated): the star-item instruction for the additive rows of clause 2 is an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: those prices cannot be formed without it. Row 280301 and rows 280301 and 280302 of Chapter 28 are cited by number as haulage payment sources; Chapter 28 is not yet audited and no rule of it is imported. The mix-design, gradation and approval requirements of clause 3 are acceptance requirements stated in the technical specifications; no test procedure, frequency or limit beyond those printed is imported. Entry 43 of the book-wide bottom-of-page English glossary falls inside this chapter (Road mix, p223); it is a terminology aid and defines no measurement or payment rule.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: group 3 carries priced rows without a written requirements section, unlike groups 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6. A Persian superscript footnote marker reading «85» is attached in clause 6 of the General Requirements (p223) with no footnote text printed anywhere in the chapter; it is content-free. Rows 270106 and 270107 print the identical price 174,500 rials; recorded as printed. The codes skip 270108 onwards, 270201, 270203 onwards, 270305 to 270309, 270311, 270321 onwards, 270401, 270405 to 270409, 270413 onwards, 270504 onwards and 270605 onwards; no missing code or price is inferred from the sequences. The Latin emulsion designations of 270102 to 270107 are set in the corrupted font; their true readings come from the glyph map (see the PDF layer note).
