All 13 rows print positive prices; there is no blank cell, no cell reading one and no negative price. The row list matches the appendix table and the transport-row section of §11 exactly. The band semantics are as printed: the road rows charge the entire distance after deducting the first 30 kilometres at the rate of the band into which the total distance falls, with more than 30 excluding 30 and up to including the named value, and 280106 printing no upper bound; the sea rows are nautical-mile bands of their own, with 280501 starting at zero.

Verified rules (VERIFIED_SPEC_ONLY), each limited to the rows or conditions named; block J remains authoritative for the calculation behaviour:

- Clause 1: the loading, transport and unloading of materials from the delivery place up to the site warehouse, up to 30 kilometres, and also from the site warehouse to the place of use, is included in the prices of the rows of the other chapters of this pricebook; haulage beyond 30 kilometres is considered, as applicable, per the rows of this chapter; for materials for which no beyond-30-kilometre haulage is provided in this chapter, no separate haulage payment is considered. Clause 2: the material procurement place must reach the consultant engineer's approval and the employer's ratification. Clause 3: the basis of determining the haulage distance is the shortest route on which the cargo vehicles can travel, per the consultant engineer's opinion. Clause 4: when the material haulage takes place on dirt and sand roads, the prices of the rows of this chapter are considered with the coefficient one and three tenths. Clause 5: the quantities and conditions set in this chapter are solely for computing the haulage costs and are not citable for computing material quantities or other matters.
- Group 1 requirements: 1-1 sets the haulage origins and the material quantities of cement, stone materials, steel, brick and block for the beyond-30-kilometre cost. 1-2 (cement): the origin is the nearest domestic cement factory producing the cement at the time of procurement (1-2-1); bulk cement haulage is considered with the coefficient one and fifteen hundredths applied to the prices of the related rows, for the whole route, after deducting 30 kilometres (1-2-2); the cement quantities of 1-2-3 to 1-2-10 (in-place concrete including the roof-slab concrete conversions, shotcrete, precast concrete parts, cement plasters, flooring and facing mortars, masonry mortars with the printed mortar-volume percentages, cement blocks and injection grout) are recorded in the transport-quantity section of §11 and are not restated here. 1-3 (stone materials): the origin is the nearest production place (1-3-1) and the tonnage factors of 1-3-2 to 1-3-13 are recorded in the transport-quantity section of §11. 1-4 (steel): the origin rules for direct purchase from domestic producers or the commodity exchange (1-4-1) and otherwise the nearest producer including rolling mills (1-4-2); galvanised cold-rolled steel materials and parts with the coefficient one and two tenths from the producing factory (1-4-3); one and five hundredths kilograms per kilogram of steel consumed in Chapters 7, 9 and 16 (1-4-4); factory-fabricated steel structures per 1-4-5, where the beyond-30-kilometre haulage from the procurement place to the fabrication factory follows 1-4-4 and the loading, transport and unloading of the fabricated parts from the factory is paid from the related row without deducting the first 30 kilometres with the coefficient 2. 1-5 (brick and block): the origin is their production place (1-5-1) and the tonnage factors of 1-5-2 to 1-5-5 are recorded in the transport-quantity section of §11.
- Group 3 requirements (3-1): for each cubic metre of compacted asphaltic concrete and factory cold asphalt, the equivalent of two and two tenths tonnes of asphalt is considered for haulage.
- Group 5 requirements (5-1): in the sea-transport rows, all costs of loading, transport, unloading and the related dues are included.

Chapter 27 cross-validation (block AH): the rows BC cited are verified as printed — 280301 is the transport of asphaltic concrete and factory cold asphalt (more than 30 up to 75 kilometres, ton-kilometre, 37,300 rials) and 280302 is the transport of factory cold asphalt (more than 30 up to 150 kilometres, ton-kilometre, 22,800 rials), consistent with Chapter 27 clause 7 and sub-clause 1-6 citing 280301 for asphaltic-concrete materials and both rows for factory cold asphalt, as applicable. Block AH needs no correction.

Thresholds and boundaries: the 30-kilometre inclusion of clause 1; the road distance bands (more than 30 up to 75, 150, 300, 450 and 750 kilometres, and more than 750 with no upper bound); the sea bands (up to 10, beyond 10 up to 30, beyond 30 up to 60, beyond 60 up to 90 and beyond 90 up to 150 nautical miles); the coefficients of clause 4, 1-2-2, 1-4-3, 1-4-4 and 1-4-5. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: the treatment of a fractional kilometre or nautical mile; the behaviour of road haulage beyond the named bands other than 280106 (which is unbounded) — none arises, since the bands are contiguous; the sea-band application method and the coefficient combination order are recorded as such in block J and are not repeated here. Beyond 150 nautical miles there is no row, per block J.

Chapters 7, 9 and 16 are named in 1-4-4 for the steel tonnage (subject references). The appendix does not mention the floor coefficient P, the site-setup percentage or price adjustment, and no interaction with them is inferred.

External references: the star-item instruction for materials outside both tables (clause 3) is the already-recorded title-only EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. The transport chapter is cited generically in clause 2 for the beyond-thirty-kilometre exception (payment reference only; block AI is the chapter audit). No standard, نشریه, General Conditions clause or other appendix is cited anywhere in Appendix 1, and no cross-appendix reference exists in it.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the appendix sequence is not numeric — the next printed unit after Appendix 1 is Appendix 4 (p239); the opening title prints «پیوسـت» with a kashida while the Appendix 4 opening prints «پیوست» without one, and the Appendix 1 running headers set the numeral without the intervening space. Table 1 lists thirteen chapters in the order five, twelve, thirteen, fourteen, sixteen, seventeen, eighteen, nineteen, twenty, twenty-one, twenty-two, twenty-three and twenty-four, with no row for chapters one to four, six to eleven and fifteen (absent from the pricebook) or for chapters twenty-five to twenty-nine; no missing row is inferred and no coefficient is assigned to an unlisted chapter. Row 411004 of Table 2 prints no price. No duplicate code, no negative price and no suspicious placeholder cell is printed, and no footnote or superscript marker and no bottom-of-page glossary entry appears anywhere in the four pages.

PDF layer note: the digits of the clauses, of Table 1 and of Table 2 were read with the glyph mappings validated in Phases 3.5-AX to 3.5-BE. The only Latin text in the appendix is the U.P.V.C of the Table 1 chapter-twenty-three row, set in Times Bold, and the profile Z of Table 2 row 411101, set in Times New Roman — both reliable fonts requiring no corrupted-font decoding; the appendix price tables set their digits in the corrupted font and every value was therefore verified at glyph level, independently confirming the rendered-image readings of the earlier phases.

Isolation: this audit changes no rule of the on-site-material blocks; the ONSITE rules, block F, the Table 1 and Table 2 sections of §11 and the mapping-verification section of §11 remain authoritative. Nothing here leaks to Chapter 28 or any chapter block, and no rule of Chapters 16 to 29 is imported. Chapter 15 stays absent (block V). No default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price.

**AL. Appendix 2 floor coefficient (Phase 3.5-BG, printed pp. 239–240, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph: «پیوست 2. ضریب طبقات» — identical wording on the opening title (p239) and both running headers (pp. 239–240), the word «پیوست» printed without a kashida, a space before the numeral and a period after it. The numeral is the digit glyph that the validated map of the header font reads as two — the same mapping that decodes the edition line on these very pages as the year 1404 — while the ToUnicode table of the same font maps that glyph to four. The block AK boundary note recorded the next unit after Appendix 1 as «پیوست 4. ضریب طبقات» and concluded that the appendix numbering is not sequential; that note reproduced the corrupted text layer, not the printed page: the printed numeral is two, so the appendix sequence is sequential — Appendix 1 (pp. 235–238), this unit (pp. 239–240) and the next unit (p241, Appendix 3) — and no non-sequential numbering exists on these pages. The Phase 3.5-BG instruction called this unit Appendix 4 after that note; the printed page says otherwise and this block records the printed title. Block AK is locked and is left exactly as written; the correction is recorded here, not applied there. The appendix numbers of the units beyond p241 that the earlier scan read from the running-header text layer are likewise unverified and must be glyph-read in their own phases before use. The floor-coefficient rules of the source register (FLOOR-01 to FLOOR-03) and the C5 coefficient contract already carry appendix number two and agree with the printed page.

Boundary: the unit is exactly two pages, pp. 239–240. The previous page p238 still belongs to Appendix 1 (its running header glyph-reads «پیوسـت1. مصالح پای کار», with the kashida and the numeral attached, and its footer reads 238), and the unit opens on p239 (footer glyph-verified 239). The p240 footer's text layer prints 241, but its glyphs read 240 — another ToUnicode corruption, recorded as printed evidence. The last content of p240 is the example's final evaluation line, and the next printed unit opens on p241 with «پیوست 3. شرح اقلام هزینه‌های بالاسری» — glyph-verified on its opening title and running header (the text layer says five; the glyphs say three) — which is the overhead-cost-items appendix already recorded at pp. 241–242. The floor-coefficient appendix therefore ends on p240 and the next printed boundary is p241, Appendix 3.

Prior coverage, unchanged and not duplicated here: the rules FLOOR-01 (clause 1, sub-clauses 1-1, 1-2 and 1-3 and Notes 1 to 3), FLOOR-02 (the Note 4 rounding) and FLOOR-03 (the clause 1-4 recalculation), the formula transcription, the variable definitions and the reference case of the floor-coefficient section of §11, and the implementation contract block B of §12. This block is the Phase 3.5-BG source-completeness, boundary and glyph-verification audit of that coverage.

Completeness audit, all read glyph by glyph: the printed inventory is exactly clause 1, sub-clauses 1-1, 1-2 and 1-3, clause 1-4, the formula (printed as an image, see below), the variable-definition list, Notes 1 to 4 and one worked example — no fifth sub-clause, no clause 2, no fifth note, no footnote, no superscript marker and no bottom-of-page glossary entry on either page. All clause and note numbers were glyph-verified; the sub-clause numbers 1-1 to 1-3 print their connector as a tatweel while 1-4 prints a hyphen, recorded as printed. Clause 1 states the motivation exactly: the listed prices are set for work on the ground floor (همکف) and the floor below it (زیر همکف), and when work is done on floors above or below those two, the floor coefficient covers the cost of transporting materials to those floors, the material waste (افت) arising from that transport and the difficulty of executing the work, and is included in the estimate of the execution cost.

Formula audit: clause 1-3 introduces the coefficient (P) — the capital P set in Times New Roman Bold inside parentheses — as obtained from the relation below, calculated separately for each building, excluding landscaping items, and applied to all work items of that building. The relation itself is not text: it is printed as an embedded raster image (721 by 57 pixels, placed on p239 between clause 1-4 and the definition list), and no text layer of it exists. Its transcription in the floor-coefficient section of §11 is therefore not re-read here; instead every glyph-verifiable element around it was checked and agrees with that transcription: the definition list defines each variable, clauses 1-1 and 1-2 state the weighting, and the example's final evaluation line prints the formula applied with numbers — one plus the quotient of the weighted-sum total over one hundred times S. A pixel-structure analysis of the image confirms a fraction bar spanning about three hundred and forty points with a long numerator above it, a markedly shorter denominator below it and prefix tokens before the fraction, consistent with the recorded structure; the literal coefficients and ellipses inside the image cannot be re-verified without reading the image itself, and no new transcription is made here.

Variables, weighting and S: the printed definition list, read glyph by glyph, defines F0 as the ground-floor (همکف) area; F1, F2, F3 and so on to Fn as the areas of the first, second, third and n-th floors above the ground floor; B0 as the area of the floor below the ground floor (زیر همکف); B1, B2, B3 and so on to Bm as the areas of the first, second, third and m-th floors below it; and S as the total building floor area, including the ground floor, the زیر همکف floor, all floors above the ground floor and all floors below the زیر همکف floor (the S line prints «زیرهمکف» joined and is set at the smaller size; the list prints dot-leader lines between F3 and Fn and between B3 and Bm). F and B are thus areas, not counts or coefficients, and each floor enters the numerator as its area multiplied by its ordinal from one upward on both sides; F0 and B0 carry no weight but are included in S — the example prints no product line for them and includes them in the S sum. The definition tokens print their Latin letters in Times New Roman Bold at the smaller size with the digits in the Persian font; the zero of F0 and B0 decodes as zero at glyph level while the text layer renders it as three. Nothing is printed about mezzanine, roof, attic, parking, service or partial floors — NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK for each — and no gross or net area basis is printed beyond «سطح زیر بنا» determined separately per floor from the approved drawings — NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK.

Rounding: Note 4, glyph-verified, prints that the floor coefficient is taken with four decimal places in the calculations; if the fifth digit after the decimal point (ممیز) is less than five it is dropped, and if it is five or more one unit is added to the fourth digit — mathematical round-half-up at the fifth decimal, matching FLOOR-02. When that rounding is applied relative to other coefficients, and any statement that each building's coefficient is rounded separately, are not printed — NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK, unchanged.

Scope, exclusions and progress statements, glyph-verified and matching the register: per-building separate calculation and application to all work items of that building (clause 1-3 and Note 3); landscaping excluded (clause 1-3) and defined by Note 2 as all building operations or mechanical and electrical installations performed outside the building; on-site materials (مصالح پایکار) excluded and application from the first progress statement (Note 3, which names the on-site materials without citing Appendix 1 by number); multiple ground levels resolved to the main ground floor specified in the initial drawings (Note 1); and the clause 1-4 one-off recalculation applied in the last progress statement, together with its entitlement-on-change rule. Note 3 says the coefficient is determined for each of the «ساختمانهای مشمول» (eligible buildings) without printing an eligibility criterion — NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK — and no shared-area, attached-building, identical-building or repeated-block rule is printed — NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK. The unit prints no overhead, regional or contractor coefficient, no price adjustment, no capital or non-capital distinction and no combination order; those questions remain as already recorded and nothing is imported from the application instructions or any other appendix.

Worked example, every number glyph-verified: a building with three floors below the زیر همکف floor and eleven floors above the ground floor; the three below-floors at 400 square metres each (total 1200), B0 = 400, F0 = 600, the first to tenth above-floors at 500 each (total 5000) and the eleventh at 400; and S = 1200 + 400 + 600 + 5000 + 400 = 7600. Fourteen product lines: 400 = 1 × B1, 800 = 2 × B2, 1200 = 3 × B3, 500 = 1 × F1, 1000 = 2 × F2, 1500 = 3 × F3, 2000 = 4 × F4, 2500 = 5 × F5, 3000 = 6 × F6, 3500 = 7 × F7, 4000 = 8 × F8, 4500 = 9 × F9, 5000 = 10 × F10 and 4400 = 11 × F11; their printed total (جمع) is 34300. The arithmetic of the fourteen lines and of S checks exactly, and no product line exists for F0 or B0. The final line prints p = 1 + 34300/(100 × 7600) = 1 + zero slash zero four five one two = one slash zero four five one (the decimal separator prints as the Persian slash). Source inconsistency, recorded and not reconciled: the printed intermediate — zero, separator, zero, four, five, one, two, five digits after the separator — differs from the exact quotient of 34300 over 100 × 7600, which is zero point zero four five one three one five with further digits and rounds to zero point zero four five one three at five decimals; it also differs from the six-decimal intermediate zero point zero four five one three two that the floor-coefficient section of §11 records as the source's printed intermediate. The final printed value one slash zero four five one does agree with the Note 4 rounding of the exact quotient. The engine is unaffected — it computes the exact quotient and rounds per FLOOR-02, and the printed intermediate is never an input — and the §11 note is left exactly as locked.

Tables, Latin and images: no numerical table is printed in this unit — the definitions and the example are typeset prose and computation lines — so NO_NUMERICAL_TABLE_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK applies, and no coefficient value, price or item code is printed anywhere in the unit. The only Latin letters are the formula variables (P, p, F, B, S, n, m) set in Times New Roman or Times New Roman Bold — reliable fonts; the lowercase p of clause 1-4 and of the example's final line versus the capital (P) of clause 1-3 is recorded as printed. The only unit-specific image is the formula; the seal-like background image on these pages appears identically on every page of the appendix range and carries no content.

External dependencies and isolation: the unit cites no circular, standard, other appendix or external document, and no external value is printed or needed here. The regional-coefficient circular dependency (circular 94/69416 dated 1394/04/30 with its annex and amendments) belongs to the regional-coefficient appendix recorded at p243 in the regional-coefficient section of §11 and is not part of this floor-coefficient unit; the association of that circular with this unit in the phase instruction arose from the text-layer appendix misnumbering corrected above, and no regional or overhead value is printed or needed here. This audit changes no rule: FLOOR-01 to FLOOR-03, the floor-coefficient section of §11, block B of §12 and C5 remain authoritative; block AK is left exactly as locked with its correction recorded here; nothing leaks to another appendix or chapter; and no default rounding, combination, extrapolation or price is introduced.

**AN. Appendix 4 regional coefficient (Phase 3.5-BI, printed p243, every line read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph: «پیوست 4. ضریب منطقه» — the word «پیوست» without a kashida, a space before the numeral, a period after it, and the last word printed as «منطقه», without the «ای» suffix, on both the running header and the opening title, which are otherwise identical in wording. The appendix numeral is the digit glyph that the validated map of the header font reads as four — the same mapping that decodes the edition line on this very page as the year 1404 — and here the ToUnicode table happens to agree, printing four in the text layer as well; on the pages before it the same table lied, so the per-glyph corruption is erratic and text-layer digits remain non-evidence throughout. The body clauses print the coefficient name with the suffix, «ضریب منطقه‌ای», while the title prints it without — recorded as printed and not reconciled. The previous two blocks cite the p243 title with the suffix, following the register wording; that citation is corrected here — the printed title is «ضریب منطقه» — and both blocks are locked and left exactly as written, the same protocol the floor-coefficient block applied to the appendix-numbering correction before it.

Boundary: the unit is exactly one page, p243, its footer glyph-verified (243). The previous page p242 belongs to Appendix 3 (previous block; not re-audited). The page's content ends with the last variable definition (the Cn line); nothing is printed between it and the footer — no note, no footnote, no superscript marker, no glossary entry, no table and no continuation. The next printed unit opens on p244 with «پیوست 5. دستورالعمل تجهیز و برچیدن کارگاه», its numeral glyph-verified as five on both its running header and its opening title (the text layer prints three — corrupted), its footer glyph-verified (244); that is the site-setup/removal appendix already recorded at pp. 244–253 in the coverage table and the site-setup register rows. Appendix 4 therefore ends on p243, and the appendix sequence remains sequential: one, two, three, four, five.

Prior coverage, unchanged and not duplicated here: the rule-versus-values section of §11 (the regional-coefficient section, Phases 3.5-V and 3.5-AC: the rule clauses, the external-source identity, the county/district fallback, the boundaries dependency, the estimate-preparation-date input and the dependency classification table), the register row REGION-01 (the clause texts), the coefficient contract C7, the regional-coefficient statements of the implementation contract, and the open-dependencies table row for the regional values. This block is the Phase 3.5-BI source-boundary, completeness and glyph-verification audit of that coverage.

Completeness audit, all read glyph by glyph: the printed inventory is exactly clause 1, sub-clauses 1-1, 1-2, 1-3 and 1-4, the relation printed after clause 1-4, and five variable-definition lines (R, C, C1, C2 and Cn). No clause 2, no fifth sub-clause, no note and no example is printed. All clause labels were glyph-verified, and their connectors print as hyphens — where the floor-coefficient and overhead appendices print tatweels — recorded as printed. Clause 1 states the motivation exactly: the prices in this price list are based on labour, machinery, materials and transport prices with easy access to materials and services, and to compensate the additional costs arising from the distribution of infrastructure, the climate conditions across the country, the geographical conditions, the manpower and the distance from the main centres, the regional coefficient is determined as set out below and taken into account in the estimate of the execution cost of the operations. Clause 1-1 states that the regional coefficients used in the estimate are the latest issued up to the time the estimate of the execution cost is prepared, in the annex to circular No. 94/69416 dated 1394/04/30 or its later amendments, issued by سازمان برنامه و بودجه کشور. Clause 1-2 states the fallback: if the name of the project's location is not among the regions for which a coefficient is set in that circular or its amendments, the regional coefficient of the county (شهرستان) or district (بخش) in which the project is located is used. Clause 1-3 states that the geographic boundaries of province, county and district follow the latest national divisions map published by the Ministry of Interior. Clause 1-4 states that for projects located in different regions, such as linear projects, the regional coefficient is determined by the weighted average of the relevant regional coefficients in the different regions, using the relation below. All of this agrees with the existing section and register row; nothing new is added and nothing is repeated as a new rule.

Formula audit: the relation is printed as real text — set in Cambria Math, not an image, unlike the floor-coefficient formula — and it was read glyph by glyph. It is a vertical fraction: R equals the fraction whose numerator is the sum of three parenthesised products — R one times C one, plus R two times C two, plus a midline ellipsis, plus R n times C n — and whose denominator is C. The fraction bar is a drawn rule spanning the numerator, the multiplication operator is the asterisk operator, and the ellipsis is the midline ellipsis; the leading R and the equals sign sit on the main baseline to the left of the fraction. The glyph inventory is internally consistent — the R glyph appears four times, the C glyph four times, the subscript digits one and two twice each, the n glyph twice, the asterisk three times and the plus three times — and the ToUnicode table of that font, unlike the Persian fonts of this document, maps its glyphs to the correct mathematical characters, which the glyph repetition pattern independently confirms. The printed relation agrees with the formula recorded in the existing section — R equals the sum of each regional coefficient times the estimated execution cost of the part of the work to which it applies, divided by the estimated execution cost of the work of the discipline. The five definition lines, set in Times New Roman with the Persian in the body font, define R as the regional coefficient of each discipline (رشته), C as the estimated execution cost of the work of that discipline, and C1, C2 and Cn as the estimated execution costs of those parts of the work to which the regional coefficients R1, R2 and Rn apply — matching the register row, with the per-discipline scope printed in the definitions themselves.

Numerical audit: the digit inventory of the page closes exactly — every digit glyph on p243 (thirty-seven in total) is the appendix numeral, the edition-year line, a clause label, a digit of the circular number or of the date, a formula subscript or the footer. No regional coefficient value, percentage, price, ratio or other numerical value is printed anywhere in the unit: NO_NUMERICAL_REGIONAL_COEFFICIENT_TABLE_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK. The circular number and the date are identifiers of an external source, not coefficients, and both were glyph-verified exactly as printed — the circular as the number ninety-four over sixty-nine thousand four hundred and sixteen (94/69416) and the date as 1394/04/30, with the Persian slash as the separator in both.

External dependencies, confirmed as already recorded and nothing fetched: the numerical coefficient values are the latest issued up to the estimate preparation date in the annex to circular No. 94/69416 dated 1394/04/30 or its later amendments — an EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY, since the annex is not reproduced in the 1404 PDF — and the administrative boundaries of clause 1-3 follow the latest Ministry of Interior national divisions map, recorded as a separate EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY. The amendment-currency question and the value-set provenance remain exactly as the existing section records them; no external table was accessed, no value is recorded here or in the engine, and no region-to-coefficient mapping is inferred.

Scope and interaction: clause 1 takes the coefficient into the estimate of the execution cost of the operations, and no chapter, work-type, plan-type, building-or-site or capital/non-capital restriction is printed — nothing more specific is inferred. The unit prints no multiplication or calculation order with the floor, overhead, on-site-material, site-setup or contractor coefficients — that order is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK, and the successive-multiplication wording of the application instructions is not imported here. The appendix does not mention Appendix 1, Appendix 2 or Appendix 3, the floor coefficient, on-site materials, transport or overhead costs, and nothing is imported from them. The weighted-average relation of clause 1-4 applies where a project spans several regions, as already recorded; no allocation rule beyond the printed relation is inferred.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the title prints «ضریب منطقه» without the «ای» suffix while the body clauses print «ضریب منطقه‌ای» with it, and the previous two blocks cite the title with the suffix (locked; corrected here). The next unit's numeral (p244, Appendix 5) prints five in glyphs and three in the text layer; this unit's numeral prints four in both. The clause-label connectors here are hyphens, where the previous appendices print tatweels. No clause label is missing or duplicated, and the circular reference is internally consistent with the register row.

Isolation: this audit changes no rule; the regional-coefficient section of §11, REGION-01, C7, the implementation-contract statements, the open-dependencies row and the previous blocks remain authoritative. No coefficient value, region mapping, boundary, rounding or combination order is introduced, and nothing here leaks to the site-setup appendix or any other unit.

**BJ. Appendix 5 site setup and removal instruction (Phase 3.5-BJ, printed pp. 244–253, every page read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph: «پیوست 5. دستورالعمل تجهیز و برچیدن کارگاه» — identical wording on the opening title (p244) and the running headers of all ten pages, the word «پیوست» printed without a kashida, a space before the numeral and a period after it. The appendix numeral is the digit glyph that the validated map of the header font reads as five — the same mapping that decodes the edition line on every one of these pages as the year 1404 — while the ToUnicode table of that font prints three on these pages; the corruption is erratic and glyph evidence governs, as the previous blocks record for the neighbouring appendices.

Boundary: the unit is exactly ten pages, pp. 244–253, every footer glyph-verified (244 through 253) and the header numeral reading five on all ten. The previous page p243 belongs to Appendix 4 (previous block; not re-audited). The unit consists of an introduction and four numbered clauses (pp. 244–248) followed by the row list (pp. 249–253). The next printed unit opens on p254 with «پیوست 6. کارهای جدید», its numeral glyph-verified as six on both its running header and its opening title (the text layer prints four — corrupted): that is the new-works appendix already recorded at p254 (NEW-01 to NEW-04). Appendix 5 therefore ends on p253, and the appendix sequence remains sequential from one to six. The introduction states that the instruction is prepared generically for use in the various disciplines, and that in the works of each discipline its provisions are to be used proportionately to the nature and needs of that work.

Prior coverage, unchanged and not duplicated here: the register rows SITE-01 (the cap rules of clauses 2-17, 2-17-1 and 2-17-2 with the excluded rows, the High Technical Council approval, and the clause 3-5 unchanged-lump-sum and contractor's-cost rules, together with the maximum percentage table of the application instructions) and SITE-02 (the payment rules of clause 4-1 with its note, and clauses 4-2 and 4-3), the coverage-table row for this appendix (pp. 244–253, verbatim transcription pending), the coefficient contract C8, the implementation-contract statements that site setup/removal is not a row of the estimate sums and is added after the successive coefficients, and the three existing test references. The clause texts cited by SITE-01 and SITE-02 were re-read glyph by glyph and agree with the printed pages. This block is the Phase 3.5-BJ source-completeness, boundary, table and glyph-verification audit of that coverage.

Completeness audit, all read glyph by glyph: the printed inventory is exactly the introduction, clause 1 «تعاریف» with items 1-1 to 1-13 (site setup; support buildings; public buildings; landscaping; the site entrance; the site store; the access road; service roads; communication roads; the detour road; the meaning of «تامین» in the row descriptions — providing buildings and installations by construction or installation at the site, or taking them over from existing local facilities by purchase of services or rent, together with their upkeep and operation; removal; and the site-setup layout plan), clause 2 «روش تهیه برآورد» with items 2-1 to 2-18 including the sub-items 2-17-1 and 2-17-2, clause 3 «شرایط کلی» with items 3-1 to 3-9 and one tabdare after 3-8 (the setup of administrative buildings, offices and residences belongs to the contractor after removal), clause 4 «نحوه محاسبه هزینه» with items 4-1 to 4-5 and one tabdare inside 4-1, and the row list. No clause 5, no further item, no further note and no example is printed; no footnote, no superscript marker and no bottom-of-page glossary entry appears anywhere in the ten pages. That is four clauses, forty-seven numbered items (thirteen, eighteen, nine and five, the two 2-17 sub-items included), two tabdare and the introduction.

Newly verified rules not previously recorded: clause 2-1 — the consulting engineer or estimate-preparing unit determines the most economical setup method for the conditions and needs of the work and the chosen execution method, and estimates the related costs per the rows foreseen in this appendix's list, at the prices of the place of execution, with the overhead costs included as applicable, in the unit lump sum (مقطوع), square metre or square metre-month, entering them against the related rows; the technical specifications and areas of the site offices and housing for the employer's, consultant's and laboratory staff are to be foreseen in the referral and contract documents; site setup is estimated exclusively from the rows of this appendix's table and adding rows under any title, including star rows, is not allowed; for buildings to be constructed, the salvage value of materials is deducted and the result is taken as their estimate; for prefabricated buildings such as caravans and prefabricated parts such as metal frames, the transport, installation, depreciation and investment costs over the execution period are calculated and included; and in contracts using several discipline price lists, only one setup-and-removal list is prepared for the whole work. Clause 2-2 — buildings, installations and roads within the site are considered temporary for the execution period; permanent ancillary or infrastructure installations provided for the operation period may be used as site setup, in which case their creation cost is calculated with the discipline's price lists in the execution-cost estimate and only their upkeep and operation during execution enters the setup estimate as a lump sum. Clauses 2-3 to 2-5 — the method of providing water, electricity, gas and telecommunications during execution is set in the referral and contract documents; where the employer does not take over the electricity supply or the water supply or well drilling up to the site entrance, the cost is estimated and, after deducting the returnable costs at the end of the work, the remainder is included as a lump sum. Clause 2-6 — where access, service or communication roads are not the employer's responsibility, their cost is calculated using the price list of the road, railway and airport-runway discipline and included as a lump sum. Clause 2-7 — per the General Conditions the land for site setup is the employer's; if the employer intends the contractor to provide all or part of it, the rent is foreseen as a lump sum. Clause 2-8 — any other facilities the employer intends to place at the contractor's disposal must be foreseen in the referral and contract documents. Clauses 2-9 to 2-12 — the cost of setting up workshops such as installation, smithery, carpentry, rebar-tying and prefabricated-part workshops is calculated in the unit prices of the related chapters; machinery repair workshops are in the machinery hourly cost; the work's water and electricity are in the row prices; the workers' food is in the overhead (continuing site costs), and where the contractor must bear food costs or contributions, that cost is estimated as a lump sum. Clauses 2-13 and 2-14 — where providing food for the employer's, consultant's and laboratory staff at the site is necessary, the number of users is set in the documents and the cost estimated as a lump sum; providing the vehicle needed by the employer, consultant and laboratory is not allowed in the estimate. Clause 2-15 — detour roads are not setup rows; their operations are estimated with the road, railway and airport-runway price list and their quantities enter the price list and quantities annexed to the contract. Clause 2-16 — the drawings, specifications and equipment of the buildings for the employer's, consultant's and laboratory staff, observing clause 4-4, are entered in the referral and contract documents and their cost is included in the setup estimate. Clause 2-18 — the explosives-magazine row comprises the construction of the magazine with its landscaping, fencing and ancillary buildings such as the guardhouse; protection of the explosives and the required vehicles is the contractor's, under the supervision of the responsible officials. Clause 3-1 — immediately after handover of the site the contractor prepares the setup layout plan and, after the consultant's approval, it becomes the basis of the setup. Clause 3-2 — the contractor must observe the directives of the High Technical Protection Council, the Ministry of Labour and Social Affairs, the Ministry of Health and the Environment Organization for safety, occupational-disease prevention and work, worker and workplace hygiene, and prepare a documented hygiene, safety and environment programme for the consultant's approval. Clause 3-3 — the employer introduces the contractor to the executive bodies for connections and permits. Clause 3-4 — the setup is completed within the time and specifications set in the documents, within ordinary limits for the region. Clause 3-6 — when a setup row is provided, its cost applies up to the total price foreseen in the related rows, having regard to clause 4. Clause 3-7 — the contractor insures the temporary buildings and installations it constructs against accidents such as fire and flood, from the related row. Clause 3-8 — what is built on the employer's land must be removed after the work; recyclable equipment and materials belong to the contractor (except setup performed by the employer); where the employer needs the buildings and installations, the salvage price is set at the ordinary current rate by agreement and credited to the contractor, and in that case no removal cost is allowed to the contractor. Clause 3-9 — in contracts terminated or rescinded, the buildings and installations made up to that time are treated according to the setup performed and the other conditions, per the contract documents. Clause 4-4 — at the request of the estimate-preparing unit or the consulting engineer, before the award and with the employer's approval, the consultant's costs in rows 990301 to 990304 are not inserted, and in that case they are calculated separately for the consultant under the supervision circular's provisions. Clause 4-5 — rows 991401 to 991403 are paid in proportion to the physical progress of the related operations and only if the five requirements of appendix five of Rule (ضابطه) number 773, titled «دستورالعمل ارزیابی کیفیت و مشخصات فنی عملیات اجرا شده», are satisfied.

Row-list audit: the list is titled «فهرست ردیف‌های تجهیز و برچیدن کارگاه» (p249) and has seven printed columns — شماره، نوع، شرح، واحد، بهای واحد (ریال)، مقدار، بهای کل (ریال); the column headers repeat on pp. 250–253 without the title. It prints exactly sixty numbered rows in fifteen contiguous groups, every code glyph-verified: 990101 to 990104 (contractor staff and worker housing, contractor offices, land rent), 990201 to 990202 (workers' food assistance; work clothes and protective helmet and shoes), 990301 to 990306 (housing, offices and food for the employer's, consultant's and laboratory staff per clause 4-4, high-speed internet, the closed-circuit-television head office, and the hygiene, safety and environment (HSE) and work-protection regime), 990401 to 990405 (support buildings and covered stores and the contractor's laboratory, the explosives magazine, public buildings, landscaping, the pad for holding plant species), 990501 (deep or semi-deep water well), 990601 to 990605 (site water, electricity, telecommunications, gas and fuel systems), 990701 to 990704 (access-road provision and upkeep, service roads, communication roads, and the upkeep and operation of the ancillary installations of clause 2-2), 990801 to 990802 (site transport; a boat for required inspections), 990901 to 990906 (foundations and platforms for machinery, machinery installation, machinery transport both ways, dredging equipment with tugs and pipelines, barge, tug transfer), 991001 to 991010 (scaffolding for external cladding, pile-rig transport, pile-rig relocation, pile-driving equipment transport, the casting floor for prestressed concrete beams, transport of open-web steel (پوترلانسمان) beams, relocation of prestressed-beam installation equipment, the foundation and platform for cable cranes or tower cranes or belt-conveyor concrete systems, the casting floor for weight-quay blocks or prefabricated armour, and the berthing platform or temporary jetty for sea-borne prefabricated parts), 991101 to 991107 (safety signs around trenches, pedestrian crossings over trenches, detour paths, gallery lighting and ventilation, marine lights and markers, marine buoys, tunnel-dam lighting and ventilation), 991201 to 991202 (preserving or temporarily diverting existing agricultural streams, keeping plant species at the depot until planting), 991301 to 991302 (site-setup insurance, removal), 991401 to 991403 (establishing and deploying a laboratory for earthworks, concrete and asphalt testing by the contractor), and 991501 (transport insurance of equipment in the power-line and transmission- and distribution-pole disciplines). Each typed row prints its type — first (اول), second (دوم), third (سوم) or progress-of-work (پیشرفت کار, the type of rows 991401 to 991403, wrapped over two cell lines) — and its unit: مترمربع for the area rows, مقطوع for the lump-sum rows, and مترمربع-ماه for row 991001 alone, the only row with that unit; its description limits the scaffolding to external cladding of buildings in the ابنیه discipline when the cladding height exceeds 3/5 metre — the decimal separator printed as the Persian slash — measured by the cladding area. No price, quantity or amount is printed anywhere in the list: the digit inventory of the five table pages closes exactly — every digit glyph (94, 130, 70, 86 and 32 on pp. 249 to 253) is the header numeral, the edition-year line, the footer, one of the sixty row codes, one of the five in-text clause references «بند 4-4» of rows 990301 to 990304 and «بند 2-2» of row 990704, or the 3/5 threshold — so the بهای واحد, مقدار and بهای کل columns print no numeric glyph at all. That is the printed design of the form, not missing data: clause 2-1 has the estimator enter the prices of the place of execution against the rows, so no price or quantity is recorded or inferred here for any row, and none may be imported from any other source. The list closes with one unnumbered total row, «جمع هزینه تجهیز و برچیدن کارگاه», unit مقطوع, likewise without values.

Numerical audit: the payment percentages — seventy and thirty for constructed type-one buildings, thirty and seventy for prefabricated type-one, and fifteen and eighty-five for the rental and purchased-service rows named in the 4-1 tabdare (990101, 990102, 990103, 990301 and 990302) — are glyph-verified on p248 and agree with SITE-02. No other percentage, coefficient, price or money amount is printed in the unit, and the cap percentage itself is not printed in this appendix: clause 2-17-1 refers to the table in the application instructions (the p2 table already recorded with SITE-01), and the appendix prints none of its values. The cap exclusion of clause 2-17 — rows 990104, 990301 تا 990303 and 991001 تا 991104, which are themselves to be included as lump sums — is glyph-verified and agrees with SITE-01.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the text layer prints three for the glyph five on all ten pages of this unit and four for the glyph six on the p254 opening of the next unit. The sub-clause label connectors are mixed — items 1-7, 1-13, 2-17-2, 2-18 and 3-9 print hyphens while every other sub-clause label prints a tatweel — the same typographic mixing the previous appendices print. Rows 991401 to 991403 print the type «پیشرفت کار» in their type cells (glyph-verified at content-stream level: «پیشرفت» on the first cell line, «کار» on the second), which agrees with clause 4-1’s statement that each row’s type is recorded in the table and with clause 4-5’s progress-and-quality payment condition; the earlier reading of those cells as empty is corrected by the glyph evidence, and the printed type is recorded on each row. The cap-exclusion range of clause 2-17 (990301 تا 990303) and the consultant-cost range of clause 4-4 (990301 تا 990304) differ: row 990304 lies in the latter only; both are recorded as printed. The total row carries no code. And a single raised Latin glyph — Times New Roman Bold, its ToUnicode table corrupted, its pixel shape a short horizontal stroke — prints at the end of the total row's description before its unit; no footnote or note text anywhere in the unit corresponds to it, and its meaning is NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK, recorded as printed.

External dependencies, identities recorded exactly as printed and none fetched: the General Conditions of Contract (شرایط عمومی پیمان, named in clauses 2-7, 2-8 and 3-5; edition not printed — the already-recorded open question); the directives of the شورای عالی حفاظت فنی, وزارت کار و امور اجتماعی, وزارت بهداشت and سازمان محیط زیست (clause 3-2; no document numbers printed); the price list of the رشته راه، راه‌آهن و باند فرودگاه discipline (clauses 2-6 and 2-15; a separate price book, not this PDF); the supervision circular (بخشنامه نظارت, clause 4-4; no number or date printed); and ضابطه number 773, appendix five, «دستورالعمل ارزیابی کیفیت و مشخصات فنی عملیات اجرا شده» (clause 4-5; the rule number is printed, the document itself is not in the project). **BK. Appendix 6 new works (Phase 3.5-BK, printed p254, every line read glyph by glyph).**

Title, read glyph by glyph: «پیوست 6. کارهای جدید» — identical wording on the running header and the opening title, the word «پیوست» printed without a kashida, a space before the numeral and a period after it, then «کارهای جدید». The appendix numeral is the digit glyph that the validated map of the header font reads as six — the same mapping that decodes the edition line of the page as the year 1404 — while the ToUnicode table of that font prints four on both occurrences; glyph evidence governs, as the previous blocks record for the neighbouring appendices.

The previous page p253 belongs to Appendix 5 (previous block; not re-audited). The next printed page, p255, is the document's last page (the PDF ends there): it is the closing acknowledgment «تشکر و قدردانی», set in different fonts (B Nazanin, Calibri and Cambria, none of the price-book fonts), without a running header, without a footer number, without any digit glyph of the price-book fonts and without images or drawings — back matter that names the working group and carries no rule. Prior coverage, unchanged and not duplicated here: the register rows NEW-01 (clause 1 — no unit price or quantity in the annexed list, new price per clause ج of Article 29), NEW-02 (clause 2 — reuse of the listed price with the contract coefficients and the cap with Article 29(a) increases), NEW-03 (tabdare 1 — equipment-only new work, overhead coefficient only) and NEW-04 (tabdare 2 — extra site setup, cap on the lump-sum amount), the implementation contract C9, the implementation rows of NEW-01 to NEW-04, the new-works section of the implementation contracts with its Article 29 dependency note (which relied on a visual recheck of p254), the open-dependencies row for Article 29, the coverage-table row, and the existing tests. All four register statements were re-read glyph by glyph and agree with the printed page. This block is the Phase 3.5-BK source-completeness, boundary, numerical and glyph-verification audit of that coverage; it supersedes nothing and records the clause texts in full for the first time.

Completeness audit, all read glyph by glyph: the printed inventory is exactly an introduction, two numbered clauses and two tabdare. The introduction: if, within the framework of the subject of the contract (در چارچوب موضوع پیمان), new works are instructed (ابلاغ) to the contractor, their prices are determined as follows — the framing «within the framework of the contract subject» is printed and was not previously recorded. Clause 1: if in the price list and quantities annexed to the contract (برآورد هزینه اجرای کار — the estimate of the execution cost) no unit price or quantity is foreseen for the instructed new work, the new price is determined in accordance with clause ج of Article 29 of the General Conditions of Contract — the clause letter ج is glyph-verified. Clause 2: if for the instructed new work a unit price and quantity are foreseen in the annexed price list and quantities, or the method of determining its unit price is explicitly stated in the chapter requirements (الزامات فصلها), then for the new price exactly (عیناً) that price is used, with the application of all the coefficients stated in the contract (such as the related overhead costs, the contractor's proposed coefficient and, as the case may be, the other related coefficients); and the maximum total amount of these rows, taking into account the increases of the work quantities in accordance with clause الف of Article 29 of the General Conditions of Contract, is up to twenty-five percent of the initial contract amount — the clause letter الف is glyph-verified. Tabdare 1: if the instructed new work is solely the purchase of equipment, only the overhead coefficient 1/14 is applied to it — the three digits one, one and four with the Persian slash as the printed decimal separator, the same separator convention the regional-coefficient block records for the circular identifier. Tabdare 2: if executing the works of this appendix requires new equipment and, in consequence, additional site setup beyond the site setup foreseen in the contract's documents, the additional setup items and their cost are agreed with the contractor; the additional setup-and-removal cost may be agreed up to a maximum of twenty-five percent of the contract's lump-sum site-setup-and-removal amount. No clause 3, no definitions section, no table, no formula, no example, no footnote, no superscript marker and no glossary entry is printed; the only image on the page is the standard masked logo and the only drawings are the header and footer rules common to every page. Every Latin-font glyph on the page is a space (two of them at a half-point size — typesetting artifacts): the unit contains no Latin term.

Numerical audit: the digit inventory of the page closes exactly — twenty-four digit glyphs in total, each one of: the running-header numeral (six), the edition-year line (1404), the opening-title numeral (six), the two clause labels (one and two), the Article number in each clause (29 and 29), the two cap values (25 and 25), the three coefficient digits of 1/14, the two tabdare labels (one and two), and the footer (254). No price, monetary value, quantity, row code, year other than the edition line, or coefficient other than 1/14 is printed anywhere in the unit, and no percentage other than the two twenty-five-percent caps. The two caps share one numeral but not one base — the first is a percentage of the initial contract amount and the second of the lump-sum site-setup-and-removal amount — and they are already recorded as distinct rules (NEW-02 and NEW-04); the star-row caps of the application instructions are a separate concept and are not mentioned here. Formula audit: none is printed — the unit carries no mathematical notation at all. Table audit: none is printed — the appendix has no row list, no prices and no units to extract, and nothing is inferred.

Cross-references and external dependencies, identities recorded exactly as printed and none fetched: clause ج of Article 29 of the شرایط عمومی پیمان (clause 1) and clause الف of Article 29 of the شرایط عمومی پیمان (clause 2) — the General Conditions of Contract, article twenty-nine, clauses ج and الف; no edition, document number, date or issuer is printed, the article's text is not reproduced in the PDF, and both remain EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY with their contents unrecorded — no wording, memory or external text of Article 29 is imported, and the new-price determination under clause ج is not computed by the engine. The chapter requirements (الزامات فصلها) are the requirements sections of this price book's chapters — an internal reference to no chapter in particular. The annexed price list and quantities and the coefficients stated in the contract are contract documents, not price-book content. In the reverse direction, Appendix 5 clause 3-5 cites «تبصره دو پیوست کارهای جدید» — the second tabdare of this appendix — and that target is verified to exist with matching content. The appendix does not cite Appendix 1 to 5, any chapter by number, the floor, regional or overhead-coefficient appendices, on-site materials, or the star rows.

Scope audit: the unit covers only the pricing of new works instructed within the framework of the contract subject — the split between reusing a listed price with the contract coefficients (clause 2) and determination under Article 29 clause ج when nothing is listed (clause 1), the twenty-five-percent cap that joins the new-work rows with the Article 29 clause الف quantity increases, the equipment-only coefficient, and the extra-setup agreement with its own cap. NOT_SPECIFIED_IN_1404_PRICEBOOK: a definition of «کار جدید»; the distinction between a new item, a new unit price, a quantity increase, extra work, omitted work, revised work or supplemental work beyond what clause 2 itself joins together; how the quantities of a new work are determined; how a price under Article 29 clause ج is built; whether the twenty-five-percent cap is checked per instruction or cumulatively over the contract, and what happens when it is exceeded; the meaning of «مبلغ اولیه پیمان» beyond the printed words; and any approval workflow other than the Article 29 reference — none of these is printed, none is inferred, and no execution practice fills them. The conjunctions are recorded exactly as printed: clause 1 conditions on no unit price یا (or) quantity being foreseen, clause 2 on a unit price و (and) quantity being foreseen یا (or) the unit-price method being stated in the chapter requirements — the printed یا/و logic stands as printed and its edge scope is not resolved by inference.

Source inconsistencies, recorded and not reconciled: the text layer prints four for the glyph six on both the running header and the opening title, four for every digit of the edition line 1404, and four for every digit of the footer 254 — the most completely corrupted digit layer of any unit audited so far, and the reason every number above is glyph-verified. The first word pair of tabdare 1 prints run together, «چنانچهکار», with no space glyph between the two words (glyph-verified). Tabdare 2 prints a double space between «به» and «تجهیز» (two consecutive space glyphs — glyph-verified). The tabdare labels print only a closing parenthesis — «تبصره 1)» and «تبصره 2)» — with no opening parenthesis. The page prints no zero-width non-joiner anywhere, so compound words print joined (the same typographic plainness the earlier units show). None of these is corrected or normalised; all are recorded as printed.

Existing-coverage reconciliation: already covered — the four register statements, their classification, the two caps as validation-only, the equipment-only coefficient gate, and the Article 29 external dependency. Newly verified here — the exact one-page boundary with the acknowledgment page identified after it, the full clause texts, the contract-subject framing of the introduction, the clause letters ج and الف, the printed form 1/14 with the Persian slash, the closing-evidence that no table, formula, price or Latin term exists, and the typographic inconsistencies listed above. Missing or incomplete — the verbatim transcription of the unit remains pending in the coverage table, and the contents of Article 29 clauses الف and ج remain external and unrecorded; no discrepancy with the prior coverage was found.

Isolation: this audit changes no rule; NEW-01 to NEW-04, C9, the implementation rows and contracts, the open-dependencies row, the coverage-table row and the previous blocks remain authoritative. No price, rate, quantity, cap value, workflow state or Article 29 content is introduced, and nothing here leaks to any other unit. The document's remaining page is acknowledgment back matter and is not audited beyond its identity.

### 12.5 Engine safety contract (normative)

1. Never silently substitute a missing coefficient, value or rate.
2. Never treat an unresolved value as zero or one.
3. Never treat a missing rate (for example 411004) as verified.
4. Never apply a coefficient outside its documented scope.
5. Never turn a descriptive rule (type E) into a numeric rule.
6. Never infer the Appendix 1 ↔ Chapter 28 mapping.
7. Never use transport quantities as takeoff or consumption quantities.
8. Never generalise 1.14 beyond OVERHEAD-02, GENERAL-10-01 and NEW-03.
9. Never generalise the 6% cement addition beyond Chapter 28 transport tonnage.
10. Never generalise the 1.05 steel factor beyond Chapter 28 transport of steel in chapters 7, 9 and 16.
11. Never assume a treatment for concrete voids > 0.05 m³.
12. Never fabricate regional coefficients.
13. Never fabricate star-row pricing or approval rules.
14. Never turn a cap (4%, 25%, 30/15/10%) into an automatic charge.
